Indonesian Shares Trade at 1-Month High — Indonesian equities extended their winning streak into a seventh session Monday, with the IDX Composite rising 56 points or 0.9% to 6,231, hitting the highest level in a month. Gains were broad-based, led by property, energy, basic materials, and transport, underpinned by strong investment momentum. Foreign direct investment surged 27.4% yoy to a fresh record in Q2 2026, reinforcing sentiment. In its main trading partner, China, the central bank held loan prime rates steady for the 14th month in its July fixing, balancing growth support with currency stability. However, upside was tempered by caution ahead of Bank Indonesia’s policy meeting this week, following 100bp of rate hikes in May–June to shore up the rupiah. Globally, Dow futures slipped as Gulf tensions lifted oil and reignited inflation worries, despite softer U.S. CPI data last week. Standout movers included Petrindo Jaya Kreasi (4.0%), Bank Rakyat Indonesia (3.0%), Indah Kiat Pulp & Paper (2.6%), and Medco Energi (2.4%).

【MIIT to continue growth-stabilisation plans for machinery, autos and power equipment, expand high-quality supply and effective demand】China’s Ministry of Industry and Information Technology (MIIT) will continue a new round of growth-stabilisation measures for machinery, automobiles and power equipment and push implementation of the Smart Connected NEV Industry Development 15th Five-Year Plan to expand high‑quality supply and effective demand. MIIT will speed commercial rollout of domestic equipment — including machine tools, industrial robots and medical devices — and accelerate first‑of‑kind major equipment deployments. 【工信部：持續實施新一輪機械、汽車等行業穩增長工作方案，全方位擴大優質供給和有效需求】7月20日，國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會，介紹2026年上半年工業和資訊化發展情況。工業和資訊化部總工程師王衛明表示，上半年，裝備工業出口交貨值同比增長18.2%, 對工業出口增長的貢獻率達到了近50%。下一步，工信部將全力推動裝備工業轉型升級。一是推動行業實現平穩增長，緊扣“十五五”規劃，落實各項統籌部署，做好智慧網聯新能源汽車“十五五”發展規劃宣貫和落實，持續實施新一輪機械、汽車、電力裝備等行業穩增長工作方案，全方位擴大優質供給和有效需求；二是加快關鍵核心技術攻關，統籌推進產業技術再造和重大技術裝備攻關工程，持續提升重點產業鏈供應鏈韌性和安全水平；三是加快國產裝備推廣應用，深入實施工業母機、智慧機器人、醫療裝備等行業的供需對接，加快首臺（套）重大技術裝備推廣應用，做好新能源汽車下鄉活動，持續開展智慧網聯汽車車路雲一體化應用試點准入和上路通行試點，啟用大宗商品市場消費潛力。

【MIIT: Building next‑generation communication networks a priority for China's telecom sector】China's Ministry of Industry and Information Technology said it will prioritise planning and construction of next‑generation communication networks, focusing on three areas. First, accelerate deployment of foundational, space, international and converged networks—scale commercial roll‑out of 5G and 10‑Gbps optical networks, expand international subsea cable connectivity, and speed development of new industrial networks to support an intelligent economy. Second, strengthen industry support for core technologies: step up R&D in 6G mobile, optical communications, subsea fibre and satellite communications, accelerate related product development, and coordinate enterprise‑led innovation with industry platforms, research institutes and associations to align innovation and supply chains. Third, drive application innovation and adoption: deepen 5G and gigabit‑fiber use in key industries, promote new application scenarios and integrate brain‑computer interfaces, embodied AI and intelligent connected vehicles with 5G/6G/satellite and internet, using next‑generation networks as a digital base to enable smart water systems, new power grids, urban utility networks and logistics network integration.

【MIIT: AI smartphones, PCs, smart robots, AI glasses and AI toys emerge as new information-consumption hotspots】China's Ministry of Industry and Information Technology said 5G base stations totalled 5.102 mln at end-June and 10G-PON ports capable of gigabit service reached 32.86 mln. Authorities are accelerating 5G-A commercial network rollout and have piloted 10G-PON deployments in residential compounds, factories and industrial parks. 5G-based smart terminals are diversifying; MIIT identified AI smartphones and PCs, smart robots, AI glasses and AI toys as new information-consumption hotspots. The ministry approved frequency-use permits in the 6GHz band for 6G trials and is advancing second-phase 6G technology tests. Openness measures are expanding: a pilot widens foreign access to value-added telecom services and foreign-invested telecom firms total 3,219, up 23.9% YoY.

### 2026-07-20 10:25:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

China 10Y Yield Rises After PBoC Decision — China’s 10-year government bond yield rose toward 1.74% on Monday, rebounding from the previous session even as the People's Bank of China kept its key lending rates at record lows for a fourteenth straight month in July. The PBoC left the one-year loan prime rate, the benchmark for most corporate and household loans, unchanged at 3%, while the five-year LPR, a reference rate for mortgages, remained at 3.5%. The decision came despite softer-than-expected Q2 GDP data that highlighted the uneven nature of China's recovery. While manufacturing activity and exports remained resilient, weak domestic demand and subdued household consumption continued to weigh on broader economic momentum. Investor attention is now shifting to the upcoming Politburo meeting later this month, a closely watched event that could provide clues on additional fiscal and monetary support measures as authorities seek to boost domestic demand, stabilize the property sector, and support economic growth.

### 2026-07-20 10:23:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Currency #Importance 1

Offshore Yuan Hovers Near 1-Week Low — The offshore yuan hovered around 6.77 per dollar on Monday, remaining near a one-week low as investors weighed escalating tensions in the Middle East and the People's Bank of China's decision to leave key lending rates unchanged. Sentiment remained fragile after the US launched fresh airstrikes against Iran over the weekend, while Tehran declared its ceasefire with Washington no longer in effect. On the monetary policy front, the People's Bank of China left its key lending rates unchanged at record lows for a 14th consecutive month in July, with the one-year loan prime rate (LPR) held at 3.0% and the five-year LPR at 3.5%. The decision came despite softer-than-expected second-quarter economic data that underscored the uneven nature of China's recovery, as robust manufacturing output and exports continued to provide support while sluggish consumer spending weighed on growth. ### 2026-07-20 10:19:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Yen Trades Near 40-Year Lows — The Japanese yen traded around 162.3 per dollar on Monday, hovering near its weakest level since 1996 as a stronger dollar and surging oil prices, driven by the escalating conflict in the Middle East, continued to weigh on the currency. The US military carried out fresh airstrikes against Iran following the deaths of three American service members, while Tehran said its ceasefire with the US had effectively collapsed and that it intercepted four vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz over the weekend. Japan remains heavily dependent on oil imports from the Middle East, leaving the yen particularly vulnerable to supply disruptions and higher energy costs. Investors also saw little indication of decisive action from Tokyo to support the currency, while awaiting intervention data due later this month for clues on whether Japanese authorities were behind the sharp but short-lived yen rallies seen in recent weeks.

【摩根士丹利下調韓國綜合指數悲觀目標位至 6000 點，理由是投資者情緒低迷】摩根士丹利將韓國綜合股價指數（Kospi）悲觀情景目標位從 6500 點下調至 6000 點，稱該指數今年早些時候的大幅衝高讓眾多投資者心生恐慌、高處不勝寒。包括尹俊碩在內的分析師在研報中寫道：“槓桿型交易所交易基金（ETF）拉動韓國綜合指數與晶片板塊持續走高，市場已顯現交易疲憊。”摩根士丹利維持韓國綜合指數基準目標 9000 點不變，同時下調該指數 3 至 6 個月區間波動區間至 6000–9000 點。行業配置方面，該行上調通訊服務板塊至超配評級；將銀行板塊列為首選，其次是資訊科技、工業製造與醫療保健板塊。工業製造板塊內部，國防軍工為首選，緊隨其後的是造船廠、發電 / 核電、海外工程承包板塊。“從中長期市場視角來看，市場行情輪動擴散是大勢所趨；但短期內，除非改革帶來估值重估，或是工業製造板塊強勢拉昇，否則僅靠行情擴散難以顯著扭轉大盤走勢。”

### 2026-07-20 10:05:52  #MKT News

【Morning tech rebound: repair after leverage-driven liquidation or genuine reversal?】Early-session tech strength looks like a post-liquidation sentiment repair rather than a resumption of a one-way AI hardware rally. Refinitiv shows SOXL and other 3x long semiconductor ETFs are off more than 50% from late-June highs; Goldman Sachs flags that retail margin, leveraged-ETF AUM and short-dated options volumes had jointly elevated market leverage. Leveraged-ETF mechanics — passive buying on rallies and forced selling on declines — amplify semiconductor-stock volatility. The earlier ChangXin Technology IPO also raised concerns; the Sci‑Tech Innovation 50 (STAR 50) is nearly 20% below its early‑July peak, indicating liquidity has been drained from high‑beta tech. For a durable recovery markets need two confirmations: 1) overseas semiconductor leveraged funds stop net outflows and instruments like SOXL show volatility convergence; and 2) the A-share tech bounce evolves from an oversold repair into sustained rotation backed by turnover and earnings.

Platinum Falls Below $1,600 — Platinum futures fell further below $1,600 an ounce, moving near late-November lows as escalating attacks in the Middle East kept inflation concerns in focus. Hostilities between the US and Iran have expanded beyond military targets to critical infrastructure, while Tehran declared its ceasefire with the US had effectively collapsed. Shipping through the Strait of Hormuz has largely come to a halt, driving oil prices sharply higher and raising the risk that central banks may tighten policy to contain inflation. Meanwhile, softer-than-expected US consumer and producer inflation data prompted markets to scale back expectations of a near-term Fed Reserve rate hike, offering some support to non-yielding assets such as platinum. The metal also remained underpinned by a tight supply outlook, with the World Platinum Investment Council continuing to forecast a fourth consecutive market deficit in 2026 as demand outpaces supply and above-ground inventories remain near historically low levels.

China Stocks Rebound on Market Support — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.7% to 3,792 on Monday, while the Shenzhen Component gained 1.8% to 13,960, rebounding from last week's sharp losses as authorities ramped up efforts to shore up investor confidence and stabilize the country's struggling equity market. China Reform Holdings and China Chengtong Holdings said they boosted holdings of Chinese stocks and vowed further purchases to help stabilize markets. Meanwhile, state media reported that the securities regulator will meet market participants on Monday to discuss measures to support the stable development of capital markets. On the monetary policy front, the PBoC left its key lending rates unchanged at record lows for a 14th straight month in July, with the one-year loan prime rate steady at 3% and the five-year LPR at 3.5%, despite weaker-than-expected Q2 GDP data highlighting the uneven recovery. Hong Kong Stocks Recover After Selloff — The Hang Seng Index surged 2.3%, or 551 points, to 25,115 on Monday, rebounding from Friday's sharp losses as technology and consumer shares led a broad-based rally despite lingering geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Sentiment improved after Beijing stepped up efforts to stabilize its stock market, with two major state-backed funds disclosing fresh equity purchases and regulators preparing to meet key industry participants, boosting confidence in Chinese assets. Bargain hunting following last week's global tech-led selloff and optimism over corporate earnings further supported risk appetite, although investors remained cautious over geopolitical risks and their potential impact on energy prices and global inflation. 【上海：上半年全市貨物進出口總額達到2.55萬億元 創歷史同期新高】上海市統計局釋出2026年上半年上海市國民經濟執行情況解讀，上半年，全市固定資產投資同比增長6.8%。主要領域中，工業投資、城市基礎設施投資、社會事業投資同比分別增長19.2%、18.8%、19.5%。主要行業中，在三大先導產業蓬勃發展帶動下，智慧模型、算力平臺等領域裝置更新持續加大投入，拉動資訊傳輸、軟體和資訊科技服務業投資同比增長1.4倍。貨物進出口總額創歷史新高。上半年，全市貨物進出口總額達到2.55萬億元，創歷史同期新高。其中，進口額、出口額同比分別增長17.4%、20.1%。高技術產品是重要的增長動能，進出口總額6621.4億元，同比增長21.6%，其中，電子資訊、高階裝備產品進出口分別增長20.7%、26.5%。

### 2026-07-20 09:43:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Hit More Than 2-Month Low — The benchmark KOSPI fell more than 3% to around 6,605 on Monday, extending losses to its lowest level since late April, as weakness in global semiconductor stocks weighed on investor sentiment. The decline followed another week of losses on Wall Street, where semiconductor shares remained under pressure amid lingering concerns over AI-related spending and valuations, weighing on broader market sentiment. Escalating Middle East tensions also dampened investor sentiment following continued US military strikes against Iran, fueling concerns over disruptions to energy supplies and pushing oil prices higher. Losses were seen in Samsung Electronics (-1.6%), Hyundai Motor (-4.4%), KB Financial Group (-3.9%), and Kia Corporation (-5.1%). Meanwhile, Samsung Biologics agreed to acquire Switzerland's PolyPeptide Group in a $1.8 billion all-cash deal, the largest acquisition in South Korea's biopharmaceutical industry, expanding its global CDMO and peptide manufacturing capabilities.

【廣西已啟動災後農房恢復重建 2027年春節前基本完成】記者從廣西壯族自治區住房城鄉建設廳瞭解到，廣西已啟動災後農房恢復重建工作，將按照“科學統籌、分類施策、嚴控質量、政策幫扶”的工作安排，分階段梯次推進重建，分類選擇修繕加固、原址重建、異址新建三種恢復重建方式，規範化落實修繕補助、重建補助、臨時安置保障等政策，2027年春節前基本完成受災住房恢復重建任務，儘快恢復城鄉居民正常生產生活秩序。據悉，受本輪臺風強降雨影響，廣西受災農村房屋約5.2萬戶，南寧、貴港、欽州、防城港等地農房受災較為嚴重。日前，廣西壯族自治區住房城鄉建設廳抽調120餘名技術專家奔赴災區評估住房安全，目前已基本完成應急評估，約5萬戶評估為“可以居住”，已交由鄉鎮組織群眾陸續回搬。對於初步判定為“不可以居住”和群眾認為存在隱患的受災農房，廣西已進一步開展安全鑑定，截至7月18日，共鑑定農房3891戶，其中安全住房1293戶、一般損壞的區域性危房1639戶、嚴重損壞或倒塌的整體危房959戶。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-20 09:41:46  #其他

【《智慧體服務健康發展的法治十原則》白皮書釋出】今天（20日），2026世界人工智慧大會系列分論壇——人工智慧法治論壇舉辦。聚焦“智慧體發展的法治回應”這一主題，與會嘉賓開展了深度討論。論壇現場釋出了《智慧體服務健康發展的法治十原則》白皮書。白皮書搭建三維治理邏輯：一是覆蓋智慧體研發、訓練、執行、迭代至退出全流程，針對自主決策、跨域互動特點建立閉環風險管控機制；二是透過服務標識、分層告知同意、最小許可權、可信輸出等規則，全方位保障使用者數字權益，重點保護老人、未成年人等數字弱勢群體；三是明晰產業權責，推動智慧體互聯互通，嚴守“人類最終擔責”底線，杜絕企業借智慧自主性逃避責任。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-20 09:41:31  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【White paper: Ten Legal Principles for Healthy Development of AI Agent Services published】At the World Artificial Intelligence Conference’s AI Rule of Law Forum on July 20, 2026, organizers published a white paper titled Ten Legal Principles for Healthy Development of AI Agent Services, setting a three-pronged governance framework. First, rules cover the full lifecycle—R&D, training, operation, iteration and decommissioning—and call for closed‑loop risk controls tailored to autonomous decision‑making and cross‑domain interaction. Second, measures to protect user digital rights include service labeling, tiered notice-and-consent, least‑privilege access and requirements for trustworthy outputs, with explicit protections for the elderly and minors. Third, the paper clarifies industry responsibilities, promotes interoperable AI agent connectivity, and insists on the bottom line of “humans ultimately accountable,” barring firms from using agent autonomy to evade liability.

### 2026-07-20 09:35:28  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【A-share oilfield services jump; shipping may be next】A-share oilfield services and engineering stocks opened higher as markets price an oil-risk premium from reported disruptions to Strait of Hormuz transit. Iran says transit volumes have dropped to zero; that claim remains unverified by shipping data, but markets are already repricing supply-disruption risk across the energy chain. Oilfield services are the first movers: higher Middle East transport risk would lift crude and energy-security premia, supporting upstream E&P, oilfield services and engineering demand. If the Strait’s disruption shifts from a short-lived shock to a sustained maritime blockade, shipping would be the next sector to re-rate because of lower vessel turnaround, route diversion, port delays and higher war-risk premiums. Tankers, LNG carriers and Middle East routes could see rerouting, waiting times and compressed effective capacity, amplifying freight-rate sensitivity. Key market indicators to watch for a rotation into shipping: vessel transit volumes through the Strait, LNG rerouting data, war-risk insurance premia and Middle East freight-rate moves.

### 2026-07-20 09:35:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Jordan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Jordan Inflation Eases Slightly, Remains Near 3-Year High — The annual inflation rate in Jordan slowed slightly to 2.79% in June 2026, down from 2.83% in May. However, the latest reading remained close to the highest level since April 2023, recorded in May, driven primarily by higher fuel prices. The country raised fuel prices by 11% in April, the first increase since the Middle East conflict began, and partially adjusted fuel prices in May. Transportation costs remained the main contributor to inflation, accounting for 1.27 percentage points, reflecting the impact of rising fuel prices. Rents followed, contributing 0.76 percentage points, while oils and fats accounted for 0.28 percentage points. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose 0.14% in June, easing from a 0.55% increase in May.

### 2026-07-20 09:32:13  #國際

【美伊衝突持續升級 分析指美軍事施壓或難迫使伊朗讓步】美伊近期圍繞霍爾木茲海峽通航問題再起衝突，持續相互襲擊。有跡象表明，美國可能升級對伊朗的軍事打擊，美伊雙方此輪互襲可能持續。據美國方面17日訊息，美國和以色列訊息人士稱，美國政府已通知以色列，將向以色列增派數十架空中加油機，後續可能擴大對伊朗的軍事行動。此外，一名美國空軍退役准將18日在接受媒體採訪時表示，兩名美軍士兵在約旦遭伊朗襲擊死亡可能會成為本次衝突的一個轉折點，美國可能以此為由對伊朗展開更加密集的軍事行動。不過有分析指出，美國近期加大對伊朗打擊力度、同時威脅升級衝突，現在幾乎沒有跡象表明，這種此前未能迫使伊朗讓步的軍事戰略會在這次取得成功。路透社在近期的報道中分析認為，美伊將諒解備忘錄轉化為永久和平協議的談判程序已停滯，美國可能希望借軍事行動讓伊朗重返談判桌，但是最近雙方互相打擊的根源在於，美伊對於諒解備忘錄中關於霍爾木茲海峽控制權的問題有著不同的解讀。分析人士指出，美國可能重蹈美以伊衝突爆發時的覆轍，而無論美國施加多大壓力，伊朗領導層都不太可能向美國屈服，如果美國繼續擴大打擊範圍，伊朗可能會選擇以牙還牙。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-20 09:12:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Australia 10Y Yield Hits 6-Week High — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield climbed above 4.9%, hitting a six-week high as the escalating Middle East conflict drove oil prices higher and stoked inflation concerns. The hostilities between US and Iran have now expanded beyond military targets to critical infrastructures, while shipping through the Strait of Hormuz has largely come to a halt. The US resumed its blockade of Iranian ports, and Tehran intensified attacks on ships near the Gulf, raising fears that disruptions to energy supplies could reignite global inflation and complicate the policy outlook for major central banks. In Australia, expectations for further policy tightening remained subdued, though will be challenged by rising fuel prices. Markets assign about a 70% chance of one final increase by December, while some expect the Reserve Bank to begin cutting rates next year. Traders now await upcoming key jobs data and flash PMI surveys due later this week for further clues on the country’s economic health.

### 2026-07-20 09:08:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Interest Rate #Importance 2

China Holds LPR Rates at Record Lows for 14th Month — The People’s Bank of China kept its key lending rates at record lows for a 14th straight month in July 2026, as widely expected. The move reflected caution over the fallout from the conflict in the Middle East, while Q2 GDP growth eased to its lowest level since Q4 2022, although exports remained strongly supported by AI-related demand. The one-year loan prime rate (LPR), the benchmark for most corporate and household borrowing, was held at 3.0%, while the five-year LPR, a reference rate for mortgages, remained at 3.5%. Consumer and producer price pressures persisted amid higher energy prices and supply chain disruptions linked to the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, yuan lending in June was higher than in May, though its growth was slower than in June last year. Housing prices continued to decline in June, reflecting persistent weakness in the property sector.

Australian Dollar Holds Losses — The Australian dollar held its recent decline to around $0.698, pulling back from a recent three-week high as the escalating US-Iran conflict weighed on risk assets. The safe-haven US dollar strengthened, while oil prices climbed after hostilities expanded beyond military targets to critical infrastructures. Shipping through the Strait of Hormuz has also largely come to a halt, with the US enforcing a naval blockade on Iranian ports and Iran targeting vessels, raising fears that disruptions to energy supplies could reignite global inflation and complicate the policy outlook for major central banks. In Australia, expectations for further policy tightening remained subdued, with markets assigning about a 70% chance of one final increase by December. Some investors expect the Reserve Bank to begin cutting rates next year. Traders now await upcoming key employment data and flash PMI surveys due later this week for further clues on the country’s economic health.

【飛天茅臺五糧液普五八代領漲 白酒大單品總價創六天新高】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售均價7月20日延續反彈。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9849元，較昨日上漲9元，連續兩天回升，較7月18日創下的階段低點累計上漲44元，創下六天以來最高。縱觀全場，核心單品的漲跌分化依然顯著，終端交投情緒環比繼續回暖，總價低位修復勢頭延續。

今日11大白酒單品七漲兩跌兩平，贏家數量佔據明顯優勢，整體漲幅亦略勝一籌。上漲方面，五糧液普五八代的漲幅居前，上漲6元/瓶，連續三日自“酒價內參”去年11月有統計以來最低位處反彈，累計回升15元/瓶，重新站上790元關口；飛天茅臺上漲5元/瓶，連續四日上行，零售均價升至1758元/瓶，重新整理今年3月4日以來最高價；按照i茅臺最新的官方定價1639元/瓶計，其全國終端零售均價相對官方定價的溢價擴大至119元/瓶。精品茅臺與洋河夢之藍M6+均上漲3元/瓶，其中前者回補昨日全部跌幅，但仍未突破2400元大關；後者自近一月最低位處小幅修復，但仍處於590元關口下方。五糧液1618上漲2元/瓶，自昨日創下的近一月最低位反彈，但仍未收復820元關口；青花汾20與習酒君品均小漲1元/瓶，其中前者重返370元關口，後者連續兩日回升，再度逼近630元關口。下跌方面，古井貢古20跌幅居前，下跌7元/瓶，跌至7月以來次低位；國窖1573下跌5元/瓶，價格再度回落至近一月最低位。青花郎價格環比昨日保持不變，連續兩日維持在693元/瓶；水晶劍南春同樣保持不變，已連續四日維持在403元/瓶。

Gasoline Rises to 2-Month High — Gasoline futures in the US climbed above $3.40 per gallon, reaching their highest level since May 22, as escalating strikes in the Middle East heightened concerns over disruptions to global refined fuel supplies. Iran declared the ceasefire with the US effectively over as both sides expanded attacks beyond military targets to critical infrastructure, dimming hopes of a return to the fragile truce. Meanwhile, Ukrainian strikes on Russian energy infrastructure continued to disrupt refinery operations. Refiners in the US and other regions also shifted production toward higher-margin diesel and jet fuel, reducing gasoline output. EIA data showed gasoline inventories fell by 1.5 million barrels in the week ending July 10, leaving stockpiles about 14 million barrels below their five-year seasonal average and at their lowest level for this time of year since 2012. The tighter market lifted the US gasoline crack spread to its highest level since June 2022.

Heating Oil Hits Over 3-Month High — Heating oil futures in the US climbed toward $4.20 per gallon, reaching their highest level since early April, as intensifying strikes in the Middle East fueled fears of disruptions to global refined fuel supplies. Iran said the ceasefire with the US had effectively collapsed as both sides expanded attacks beyond military sites to critical infrastructure, signaling little prospect of a return to the fragile truce. Adding to supply concerns, Ukraine continued its attacks on Russian oil refineries after Russia imposed a diesel export ban earlier this month. Seasonal refinery maintenance and years of refinery closures across the US and Europe further constrained fuel supplies. The tighter supply backdrop pushed US refining margins to record highs. Despite a 4.6 million-barrel increase in distillate fuel inventories in the week ending July 10, EIA data showed stockpiles remained well below their five-year seasonal average.

EU Gas Prices Hit 4-Month High — European natural gas prices surged 5% to above €60 per MWh on Monday, reaching their highest level in four months, amid mounting fears of further supply disruptions as attacks between the US and Iran escalated over the weekend. The US Central Command said on Sunday that it had carried out a fresh round of strikes against Iran for the ninth consecutive night, while US allies Kuwait and Bahrain reported Iranian attacks. The conflict has widened beyond military targets to include bridges, utility infrastructure, and port facilities, increasing the risk of a return to full-scale war. Shipping activity through the Strait of Hormuz has largely come to a halt, with the US enforcing a naval blockade on Iranian ports and Iran targeting vessels it says are violating its regulations. These supply risks, coupled with stronger demand for power generation, are limiting Europe's ability to rebuild gas inventories ahead of winter.

【中信建投：科技行情尚未終結】中信建投證券研報指出，科技行情是否結束了？這是當下市場最關注的話題。覆盤歷史經驗，科技是否迎來“泡沫崩潰”，取決於三點，第一，科技的產業趨勢是否真的終結；第二，流動性是否到了非緊不可的地步；第三，傳統增長是否迎來反轉。如果不是，尚不能認為科技終結。當下對科技行情的討論和5月份對美伊衝突的關注一樣。中信建投的觀點沒有變化，全球當下正在經歷一輪供給側，科技尚未終結，地緣也是。配置上建議聚焦半年報業績確定性較強的方向，重點關注三條景氣線索：一是AI硬體上游半導體裝置、材料等核心環節，業績兌現確定性高；二是工業金屬、化工材料等週期品，兼具AI需求拉動與產能約束，盈利彈性有望釋放；三是出海優勢突出的高階製造，出口韌性支撐業績增長。

South Korea Moves to Boost Global Use of Won — South Korea announced on Sunday plans to further liberalize its currency market by allowing foreign financial institutions to borrow the Korean won through temporary overdrafts and use won-denominated bonds as collateral in financial transactions. The measures are designed to encourage broader international use of the won and support the government's goal of transforming it from a restricted local currency into a more globally traded one. The reforms build on South Korea's recent efforts to open its foreign exchange market, including the extension of dollar-won trading hours to a 24-hour trading cycle from July 6. By improving access to won liquidity and increasing flexibility in funding and collateral management, the government aims to attract greater foreign participation, deepen domestic financial markets, and enhance the global competitiveness of the Korean won.

【“含韓”港股ETF淨值跳水 半導體後市行情存在分歧】2026年6月下旬起，此前累計翻倍走強的韓國半導體板塊遭遇猛烈拋壓。三星電子、SK海力士在經歷逾半年單邊上漲後掉頭急跌，宣告估值回撥開啟。作為國內唯一佈局韓國市場的跨境ETF——中韓半導體ETF華泰柏瑞亦同步重挫，7月以來跌幅已超30%。與此同時，港股市場掛鉤上述韓企的兩倍槓桿產品上演極端“過山車”行情。這一劇烈分化走勢，將市場對AI儲存“超級週期”的信仰徹底推向分歧高點。當前韓國半導體板塊後市走勢分歧顯著。受全球資本市場流動性收緊、行業週期邊際變動、市場交易結構失衡及持續累積的板塊利空影響，韓國儲存半導體板塊估值持續承壓，行情震盪加劇。國泰基金指出，韓國市場的熔斷是一記沉重警鐘：高波動賽道搭配過高槓杆，往往是風險的根源。這也說明，短期市場價格有時並不完全等於產業基本面的變化。當持倉過度集中、資金過度擁擠、槓桿比例過高時，即便行業沒有突然惡化，市場也可能發生快速調整。

Brent Jumps as US-Iran Conflict Intensifies — Brent climbed above $90 per barrel on Monday, extending last week's gains as escalating hostilities between the US and Iran raised fears of further disruptions to oil flows from the Middle East. Iran declared that its ceasefire with the US had effectively collapsed and said over the weekend that it intercepted four vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz. ### 2026-07-20 07:15:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Current Account #Importance 0

Serbia Current Account Smallest in 7 Months — Serbia recorded a current account deficit of USD 145 million in May 2026, narrowing from USD 527 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. It was the smallest deficit since October last year, driven largely by a bigger surplus for services account, which rose to USD 314 million from USD 161 million. At the same, the goods account shortfall contracted to USD 549 million from USD 571 million. The primary income account gap also fell to USD 485 million from USD 636 million, while the secondary income account surplus expanded to USD 575 million from USD 518 million. Considering the first five months of the year, the country’s current account deficit declined to USD 655 million from USD 1,973 million in the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-20 07:15:00  #公司

【上證報頭版：市場調整未改基本面 穩預期合力正加速集結】上海證券報頭版報道，近期，A股市場寬幅震盪，前期漲幅較大的科技板塊調整尤為明顯，投資者情緒也隨之波動。從追逐熱門賽道到重新審視風險，市場敘事似乎在很短時間內發生了切換。需要看清的是，當前調整更多體現為高預期的再定價、集中持倉的再平衡和風險偏好的階段性收縮，並不意味著人工智慧產業趨勢、中國科技自立自強程序或上市公司基本面發生了整體逆轉。市場真正需要修復的，是價格與基本面之間的區域性偏離，而非對中國資產長期價值的判斷。中國資產的安全韌性，來自宏觀政策空間、完整產業體系、超大規模市場和企業盈利基礎；中國科技的長期價值，則取決於技術突破、產業轉化和商業回報。這些因素不會因海外市場幾日波動或一次交易風格的切換而消失，仍是A股價值中樞的基本盤。

Over the next 3–5 years, photonic/optoelectronic chips and other frontier technologies could further reduce per-token compute costs by roughly 50% or more by lowering latency and power per operation.

【在噴氣式飛機製造業務加速推進之際，波音公司正努力應對“不穩定”的供應商問題。】美國飛機製造商波音公司正全力應對787“夢想客機”在座椅認證及發動機供應方面面臨的瓶頸問題，以期提升該盈利機型的產量。波音商用飛機部門總裁斯蒂芬妮·波普表示，當前供應鏈仍存在一定的不穩定性，推進系統的交付將是近期的重點關注領域。在核心窄體客機方面，波音正穩步擴大737系列飛機的產能。波普透露，公司目前的重點是先將737系列月產量穩定在47架，隨後逐步提升至52架，最終目標是在未來幾年內達到月產63架。這一增產計劃對於波音改善財務狀況、償還債務，以及應對空客公司在窄體客機市場的競爭至關重要。在新型號認證與交付方面，波音也取得了積極進展。波普表示，737 MAX系列中較小型號的認證將在“不久的將來”完成，而最大型號的飛行測試也即將結束。此外，備受矚目的洲際寬體客機777X計劃在2027年向客戶交付。這些新飛機的順利上市，是波音在經歷多年虧損後產生現金流、修復資產負債表的關鍵。在適航認證方面，波音近期重新獲得了為737 MAX和787客機簽發適航證的許可權。此前，因製造環節出現質量問題，美國聯邦航空管理局（FAA）曾暫停了波音的該項許可權。認證許可權的恢復，表明波音在改善生產質量和重塑航空安全監管機構信任方面取得了階段性成果。

### 2026-07-19 20:17:25  #公司 #國際

【美高超音速導彈專案受挫：產能減半 成本飆升 】美國國會下屬的政府問責局日前釋出報告，披露美國軍方名為“常規快速打擊”的高超音速導彈專案存在一系列“重大問題”，包括產能減半、成本高漲、進度緩慢等。報告顯示，由美國洛克希德—馬丁公司作為主承包商的“常規快速打擊”專案在零部件供應、生產能力和技術方面出現問題，目前每年最多生產6至7枚，僅為原計劃12枚的大約一半。報告指出，該公司編制的操作說明“對普通工人而言，過於複雜”。這些操作說明以工程規範的形式編寫而成，提供給剛畢業的高中生或職業學校學生。負責監督生產的海軍官員認為，“指望這些工人理解（這些操作說明）是不切實際的”。生產進度緩慢的同時，成本也在大幅上升。美國海軍2020年預計，262枚導彈耗資為310億美元，目前已漲至224枚導彈花費410億美元。報告說，洛克希德—馬丁公司“必須解決重大的生產問題，以按照預期的速度和成本”推進該專案。“常規快速打擊”專案的高超音速導彈將裝備美軍“朱姆沃爾特”級驅逐艦。該型驅逐艦目前正接受相應改造。然而，政府問責局在同一份報告中說，截至今年1月，首艘“朱姆沃爾特”級驅逐艦的現代化改造工作已完成94%，但進度落後原計劃24個月。由於“資金和測試”方面存在問題，原計劃2025年開始在該艦上進行的高超音速導彈試射已被推遲至2027年。（新華社）

### 2026-07-19 20:02:38  #宏觀

【央視曝光三無網紅面膜 網紅面膜核心原料竟是違禁成分】消費者線上下美容店或線上直播間，購買使用了名為“七子白古法護膚產品”後，面部皮膚出現了不同程度的過敏現象，部分消費者甚至症狀嚴重，需要就醫治療。在許多護膚宣傳中，“七子白”被宣傳成一種從古代流傳至今的美白配方，但總檯財經記者核查《化妝品禁用植（動）物原料目錄》發現，七子白經典配方中的白芷，早已被國家列入化妝品禁用原料，嚴禁以任何形式新增在各類化妝品中。多地市場監管部門，也釋出消費警示，強調白芷屬於強光敏性中藥材，皮膚外用接觸日光，光照部位極易出現紅腫、痛癢、水泡甚至反覆溼疹，不少消費者因使用相關護膚產品造成人身傷害。 記者在短影片平臺找到了不少售賣所謂“古法護膚面膜”的直播間，這類直播間以“純手工現磨、無新增、真材實料、新鮮炮製”為宣傳亮點，大肆宣傳各種美容功效。直播畫面中可以看到，主播擺放的七味原材料裡，違禁成分白芷赫然在列，甚至是“七子白麵膜粉”的核心原料。這類銷售所謂“古法面膜”的網路直播間，大多沒有完整的生產資質，產品也沒有質檢報告，甚至也沒有正規化妝品備案，屬於典型的“三無”產品。（央視財經）

### 2026-07-19 19:45:02  #公司 #市場

【最高翻倍！6700億光通訊巨頭業績預喜，股價月內已跌超20%】7月19日，新易盛（300502.SZ）公告稱，預計2026年半年度歸屬於上市公司股東的淨利潤為70.00億元-80.00億元，同比增長77.56%-102.93%。此前，公司第一季度淨利潤為27.8億元，據此計算，第二季度淨利潤預計環比增長51%-87%。對於上半年業績變動原因，新易盛表示，主要受益於人工智慧相關算力投資持續增長及產品結構最佳化，公司銷售收入和淨利潤較上年同期大幅增加。此外，預計上半年非經常性損益對公司淨利潤的影響約為1900萬元。公開資料顯示，新易盛主營業務是全系列光通訊應用的光模組的研發、生產和銷售。值得注意的是，截至7月17日收盤，新易盛股價報482.88元/股，單日跌11.01%，總市值為6733億元；月內股價已跌超20%，創下近一年來單月最大跌幅。但這並非新易盛個股的“獨立行情”，同為光通訊巨頭的中際旭創、天孚通訊月內亦分別跌超22%、30%。當下，光通訊頭部公司股價表現與基本面出現較大程度背離，成為市場關注的焦點。就行業方面而言，國盛證券指出，當前光通訊板塊回撥，與儲存漲價擠佔雲廠商資本開支有關。具體來看，近期儲存晶片價格持續上行，儲存在整個伺服器BOM成本中的佔比上升。在雲廠商總體CapEx預算有上限的情況下，市場擔憂儲存等環節的漲價會倒逼大廠壓縮網路端（光通訊）的採購預算與毛利空間，進而影響光模組企業業績兌現。（21財經）

### 2026-07-19 18:10:10  #公司 #觀點 #焦點

【中信證券：指數行情處於一輪中期行情的休整到新一輪行情的醞釀階段】中信證券研報指出，當前有如下四個判斷：第一，指數行情處於一輪中期行情的休整（以科技板塊補跌為特徵）到新一輪行情的醞釀階段（需要新的有估值提升空間的板塊啟動），短期出清已接近尾聲。第二，北美AI鏈短期可能是科技內部的避風港，7月底北美CSP的指引前後可能迎來一波修復，但重新上一個臺階需要新一次的AI模型/產品能力躍升和商業變現空間拓展，關鍵是打破硬體公司週期股估值框架，實現硬體和應用的新一輪系統級抬升。第三，國產AI鏈高度依賴催化和趨勢資金強度，目前主要催化已陸續兌現，而趨勢資金強度在經歷劇烈回撤後很難迅速回歸，國內目前仍相對健康的融資盤意味著目前不存在籌碼出清式的佈局點，國產切北美的估值收斂是比較可能的走向。第四，非AI鏈以輪流修復為特徵，從創新藥和非銀開始，過渡到有色、化工和鋰電的工業鏈條，期間伴隨著內需鏈的政策預期交易。配置上聚焦三個維度的收斂：Al鏈上游硬體和漲價品種相對下游雲服務大廠超額收益的收斂；非AI工業股相對海外對標的公司的折價修復；科技與非科技的收斂。

【輔助駕駛兩大強制國標落地】近日，國內首個規範L2級組合駕駛輔助的強制國標GB 47955—2026（以下簡稱“新國標”）正式釋出，實施節點定在2027年1月1日。這份標準由工業與資訊化部組織制定，華為、小米、比亞迪、特斯拉等近40家車企、科技企業聯合參與編制，是約束L2級輔助駕駛產品的首部硬性法規。與此同時，駕駛輔助領域另一重磅強制國標GB 39901—2025，也敲定於2028年1月1日正式實施。 兩大國家級強制標準先後落地，標誌著國內智慧輔助駕駛將正式告別野蠻生長的時代，邁入以‌安全合規為唯一準入門檻‌的高質量發展新週期。小鵬集團董事長兼CEO何小鵬向中國新聞週刊表示：“中國的智慧輔助駕駛，無論是企業做的效果，還是相關法律法規的制定，都走在全球前列。” 新國標作為強制性標準，把產品研發、硬體配置、宣傳話術、資料留存全部納入准入稽核體系。這也意味著，誇大宣傳不再只是商業道德問題，而是觸碰法規紅線的違規行為。（中國新聞週刊）

【未來三天雲南廣西至長江中下游地區有較強降水】記者19日從應急管理部獲悉，未來三天主雨帶將維持在雲南、廣西至長江中下游地區，累計雨量大並有強對流天氣，且與前期降雨落區高度重疊，山洪、地質災害及城鄉內澇風險高。國家防總辦公室、應急管理部當日組織多部門聯合會商，研判近期強降雨發展趨勢，研究部署重點地區防汛救災工作。會商指出，廣西等前期受災嚴重地區正處在災後搶修恢復攻堅階段，救災救助與新一輪強降雨防範應對任務交織，防汛救災壓力大。強降雨區各級包保責任人必須下沉一線、在崗在位。東北退水地區要加強堤防巡查防守，排程超汛限水庫有序洩洪。廣西等前期受災地區要統籌抓好搶修恢復與新一輪強降雨防禦工作。會商強調，嚴格落實臨災預警“叫應”和跟蹤反饋機制，緊盯山洪災害危險區、地質災害隱患點、病險水庫、城市易澇點，落實“隱患點+風險區”雙控要求。充分發揮基層網格員作用，加強臨山臨江、切坡建房、山洪溝口、狹窄河道等高風險點位巡查，果斷提前組織受威脅群眾轉移避險。（新華社）

【美沙核協議草案浮出水面 搞“雙標”遭質疑】美國以摧毀伊朗核計劃等為目標對伊發動軍事行動已有數月之際，美媒18日披露的一份美國與沙烏地阿拉伯的核協議草案卻在未切實落實核不擴散制度前提下，允許美方向沙特民用核專案轉移核材料和技術。在核問題上對伊朗百般阻撓，卻給沙特開綠燈，美國“雙標”引發輿論強烈質疑。據美國有線電視新聞網（CNN）18日援引知情人士以及特朗普政府向國會提供的相關檔案報道，這份協議或價值數十億美元，包括民用核專案合作協議和雙邊強制性保障協議，前者涉及允許沙特開展民用鈾濃縮以及鈽後處理的“特殊安排”，但未說明沙特會否獲得敏感技術和材料；後者規定對“鈾濃縮、轉化、燃料製造和乏燃料後處理等最敏感的核擴散領域”採取額外的保障及核查措施，國際原子能機構（IAEA）將在核專案監督方面扮演角色，但未要求沙特履行《不擴散核武器條約》附加議定書，因而IAEA無法對沙特的核活動展開更大範圍監督。對於這一報道，沙特政府、沙特駐美大使館及美國政府均未置評。（新華社）

【AI開始走進藥房，機器人打通夜間買藥最後一公里】半夜突然發燒、腹瀉，或急需用藥，附近藥店已經關門，線上下單卻顯示“次日送達”。這樣的經歷，很多人都曾遇到過。根據相關機構調查，超過90%的居民曾在夜間有過用藥需求，超過60%的受訪者認為夜間買藥需求尚未得到充分滿足。從“無藥可買”到“及時買到藥”，深夜購藥的“最後一公里”，仍是居民健康服務中的一項現實難題。當人工智慧開始走進藥房，這一難題能否迎來新的解決方案？在2026世界人工智慧大會現場，一處名為“機器人智慧藥房”的展區前人頭攢動，圍滿了觀眾。幾臺人形機器人正在完成接單、取藥、分揀、配送全流程。記者在現場發起了一場“人機PK”，雖然最後記者比機器人先完成挑戰，但是工作人員表示，機器人真正的優勢並不是短時間內跑得更快，而是能夠24小時穩定工作。無論白天還是深夜，無論訂單多少，都能夠保持穩定效率，不會疲勞，也不會因為高峰時段而降低服務質量。有參觀者表示，機器人動作流暢、識別精準，如果未來能夠24小時值守藥房，將有效解決夜間購藥難題，也讓無人藥房擁有更多應用空間。（央視財經）

【World Artificial Intelligence Cooperation Organization to focus on three priorities】An agreement to establish the World Artificial Intelligence Cooperation Organization was signed in Shanghai on the 16th by representatives from 29 founding countries across Asia, Africa, Latin America and Europe, marking progress for a China-initiated multilateral AI body. China said it will work with founding members to start the organization’s operations soon. The body will prioritize three areas: first, international AI capacity-building — via policy exchanges, technical cooperation, personnel training and joint research — to broaden access to AI and help developing countries share in AI gains; second, acting as a supply-demand matchmaking platform to integrate national resources, promote “AI+” industry initiatives, deepen tech and industrial cooperation, encourage open-source ecosystems and produce practical cooperative projects; third, supporting the UN Charter, implementing the Global Digital Compact and the UN Sustainable Development Goals, and advancing a fair, rules-based global governance framework for AI.

After constraints in energy and power equipment, he flagged materials supply and facility construction as potential new bottlenecks — citing shortages and price rises for electrical wire and related materials and tightness in submarine cables. ### 2026-07-19 15:18:15  #國際

【俄羅斯為何攔不住無人機 俄一週攔截4830架無人機仍然攔不完 】當地時間7月10日，烏克蘭總統澤連斯基宣佈，在軍中設立“遠端打擊”特別指揮部，加強對俄羅斯的遠端打擊。近幾個月，烏軍持續擴大無人機襲擊的規模與頻率，猛烈攻擊俄羅斯重要能源和物流設施，意在“將戰火引向俄羅斯”，削弱對方的戰鬥能力。由於遭無人機襲擊，近期俄羅斯多地出現燃油短缺和供應困難，並臨時限制民用飛機起降，導致很多航班延誤。 俄羅斯國防部的統計資料顯示，從7月6日至7月12日的一週內，俄防空部隊在俄羅斯上空攔截並摧毀了至少4830架烏克蘭無人機。其中，7月8日和10日被擊落的無人機數量最多，分別為903架和821架。此前一週，至少3945架無人機遭攔截。 作為21世紀衝突中重要的打擊手段，無人機作戰已經深刻改變了軍事戰略、裝備採購和戰場動態。一邊是驚人的攔截數字，另一邊是不斷深入的打擊實效。這種看似矛盾的現象，恰恰揭示了問題的核心：現代防空體系並非“攔不住”無人機，而是無法用可持續的方式攔住足夠多的無人機。這不是某個系統的技術故障，而是攻防邏輯的結構性錯位。 烏克蘭大量使用商用現貨元件或簡易作坊式生產的自殺式無人機，單機成本僅數百至數萬美元。而俄羅斯每攔截一架這樣的無人機，往往要消耗一枚價值數十萬甚至數百萬美元的防空導彈。當俄羅斯發射一枚價值30萬美元的“鎧甲”攔截導彈去擊落一架價值5萬美元的無人機時，相當於花費6美元來節省1美元。 理論上而言，俄羅斯確實有能力擊落絕大多數來襲無人機。但現實中，絕大多數不等於“全部”，而“漏網”的那一小部分已經足以造成重大損害。更深層的困境在於，防空是一種“被動反應”行為，其效能天然受限於進攻方的節奏和規模。防禦方可以不斷提升攔截率，但只要進攻方持續擴大投放規模、降低單位成本、創新突防手段，防禦方就始終處於“追趕”狀態。

【意媒：極端高溫或致義大利每年損失120億歐元】受氣候變化等因素影響，歐洲多國的極端高溫天氣變得更常見，持續時間更長。義大利媒體18日報道稱，義大利商業、旅遊和服務業企業聯合會當天釋出的一份研究報告顯示，極端高溫出現的頻率越來越高，對企業經營和民眾日常生活造成壓力。報告指出，如果義大利每年出現30至60天的極端高溫天氣，企業將面臨能源成本上升、勞動生產率下降、被迫增加適應性投入以及營業額減少等多重壓力。這將給該國每年造成60億至120億歐元（約合人民幣465億至930億元）的經濟損失，約佔其國內生產總值的0.2%至0.4%。此外，高溫還將改變居民消費和旅遊習慣，對商業、旅遊業等受影響較大的行業造成進一步衝擊。（央視財經）

【Former Pentagon official: US strike strategy against Iran has shifted; next phase to target leadership, core military and dual-use infrastructure】Former Pentagon official David Des Roches says US strike tactics have shifted since the first phase, which aimed at decapitation and degrading Iran’s ability to conduct consecutive ballistic-missile launches. He said those strikes have reduced Iran’s ability to fire multiple missiles in rapid succession, though Tehran can still launch single or two-to-three missile shots that are usually intercepted. After Iranian attacks on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz, US strikes moved to maritime surveillance and launch nodes—including observation towers near Chabahar port that Iran has used with civilian navigation systems to fix sea targets. In recent days operations have pushed inland to isolate coastal areas and limit Iran’s ability to reinforce coasts with missiles, forces and drones. Des Roches expects the next phase to focus on Iranian national leadership, core military facilities and military-civilian dual-use infrastructure, but warned air power alone has clear limits and airstrikes rarely achieve the strategic effects claimed.

【7月美元存款利率生變，外資銀行限時加息】7月以來，部分外資銀行推出了限時高息美元定期存款活動，普遍上調各期限美元存款利率，部分產品年化利率達到4%。與之形成對比的是，國有大行、股份行、城商行的美元存款利率近期未發生調整。國家金融與發展實驗室副主任曾剛表示，近期部分銀行上調美元定期存款利率，其核心背景是7月底美聯儲議息會議臨近，市場對短期內繼續維持利率不變有一定預期，加之部分銀行有攬儲和調節外匯資金來源的需求，便透過上調美元利率吸引客戶存入外幣，緩解自身美元流動性壓力。曾剛提示，若投資者近期有明確的美元使用場景，可按需配置美元存款，減少額外匯率波動帶來的虧損風險。另外，投資者應嚴格控制配置比例與產品久期，美元資產佔個人總資產比例應控制在合理範圍內，並優先選擇3至6個月的短期美元存款，避免長期鎖定資金，以靈活應對匯率、利率變動。(中證金牛座)

### 2026-07-19 09:01:02  #市場

【飛天茅臺大漲 白酒大單品總價創五天新高】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售均價7月19日顯著反彈。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9840元，較昨日大幅飆升35元，結束此前連續七天回落頹勢，並創下五天以來最高。飛天茅臺零售均價在i茅臺平臺上調售價後快速跟漲，單日上漲35元/瓶，漲幅與今日白酒大單品總價的增量相同，成為今日總價反彈的核心驅動力。縱觀全場，核心單品的漲跌分化依然顯著，終端交投情緒環比回暖，總價自階段低點明顯修復，但低位震盪格局尚未完全扭轉。今日11大白酒單品五漲五跌一平，漲跌家數均衡，但贏家漲幅大幅勝出。上漲方面，飛天茅臺漲幅居前，大幅上漲35元/瓶，創近一月最大單日漲幅，價格升至1753元/瓶並創近一月最高價紀錄；按照i茅臺最新官方定價1639元/瓶計，其全國終端零售均價相對官方定價的價差擴大至114元/瓶。古井貢古20上漲8元/瓶，重新站上530元關口；五糧液普五八代上漲6元/瓶，連續兩日自“酒價內參”去年11月有統計以來最低位處修復，但仍處於790元關口下方；國窖1573上漲4元/瓶，回補昨日全部跌幅；習酒君品漲2元/瓶，再度逼近630元關口。下跌方面，五糧液1618跌幅居前，下跌6元/瓶，平近一月最大單日跌幅，價格降至813元/瓶並創近一月最低價紀錄，近一月其價格中樞持續下移；青花汾20與洋河夢之藍M6+均下跌5元/瓶，其中前者回吐此前兩日全部漲幅，再度跌破370元關口，後者同樣盡失此前兩日全部戰果，再平近一月最低價紀錄；精品茅臺下跌3元/瓶；青花郎小跌1元/瓶。水晶劍南春價格環比昨日保持不變，連續三日維持在403元/瓶。

### 2026-07-19 07:24:52  #其他

【四部門印發通知部署做好地質災害風險隱患巡排查工作】
記者18日從應急管理部獲悉，國家防災減災救災委員會辦公室、自然資源部、水利部、應急管理部17日印發緊急通知，部署各地區、各有關部門和單位切實做好地質災害風險隱患巡查排查和災害防範應對等工作。

通知指出，近日，甘肅宕昌縣、重慶彭水縣接連發生山體滑坡、崩塌造成重大人員傷亡，暴露出地質災害風險隱患常態化巡查排查覆蓋不全面、對災害前兆發現研判不及時等問題。

通知強調，要做好重要隱患點巡查排查，排查穩定性差、近期活動跡象明顯、近兩年監測預警裝置出現過紅色預警、切坡建房擾動、降雨為主要影響因素的在冊地質災害點。排查複核災害點（含近5年已銷號點）基本特徵、威脅物件、危險區範圍、已安裝監測裝置執行情況和已採取的防治措施及有效性。要做好重點風險區巡查排查，對轄區內已確定的中等及以上等級風險區，特別是位於城鎮、村組等重點部位的風險區，要逐一排查，要對陡坡陡崖、切坡建房等開展重點複核，對出現變形跡象的要立即納入隱患臺賬，安排監測預警等防範措施。

通知要求，針對前期降雨量大、地下水豐富、溼度變化大、旱澇急轉、人類工程活動強烈的地質災害風險區要加強防範手段，及時研判地質災害發生發展趨勢，及時透過多渠道、多形式釋出預警預報資訊。要落實好臨災預警“叫醒”“叫應”機制，果斷轉移受威脅群眾，對於老弱病殘孕幼等特殊群體落實幫扶措施，切實做到“應轉早轉”“應轉盡轉”，不落一戶一人。（新華社）

【伊拉克宣佈將重修跨國輸油管道】當地時間18日，伊拉克石油部宣佈，伊拉克和敘利亞簽署了一份諒解備忘錄，將重修一條從伊拉克通往敘利亞地中海沿岸的原油出口管道。據瞭解，這條輸油管道原建於1952年，東起伊拉克基爾庫克，西至敘利亞巴尼亞斯，全長約800公里。這條管道在兩伊戰爭和伊拉克戰爭中受到嚴重損壞，長期被棄用。伊拉克石油部表示，該專案有助於實現伊拉克原油出口路線多樣化，加強能源基礎設施建設與地區能源安全，為伊拉克原油通往全球市場創造一條新的戰略走廊。由於近期霍爾木茲海峽受阻，伊拉克原油出口大為受限。伊朗戰事爆發前，伊拉克95%的原油經霍爾木茲海峽運出，政府財政90%依賴石油收入。據英國沃泰克薩諮詢公司統計，伊拉克海運石油出口量在今年5月一度暴跌至去年同期的8%。為打通出口通道，伊拉克把目光投向這條年久失修的管道。但有分析認為，由於管道長期被棄用，需要全面維修，修好可能要花兩三年時間。（央視新聞）

【民調：近半數美國人認為最高法院裁決受政治立場左右】華盛頓郵報與益普索聯合民調顯示，超半數美國人不認可聯邦最高法院的工作表現；46%的受訪者認為，法院在審理特朗普政府相關政策案件時，依據的是意識形態而非法律條文。本月出爐的這份支援率資料，創下該院近年新低，也反映出一種長期趨勢：越來越多美國人認為法院判決帶有黨派偏向。在今年6月落幕的司法年度裡，最高法院多項裁決為特朗普政府帶來關鍵勝利——擴大總統行政權力、允許政府驅逐數十萬在美合法居留移民。但在其他核心政策議題上，法院也曾駁回特朗普的訴求：例如維持落地出生公民權、叫停特朗普對全球多國商品加徵大範圍關稅的計劃；法院還裁定，至少現階段總統無權解僱美聯儲董事會內的民主黨籍委員。這份新民調顯示，55%的美國人對最高法院履職持負面評價，僅41%表示認可。該數值徘徊在歷史低位，但尚未跌至2022年的不滿峰值。當年蓋洛普民調資料顯示，58%民眾不認可法院工作，僅40%持正面態度。

【CITIC Securities: Commercial space sector outlook improves as market risk appetite recovers】CITIC Securities research says Chinese reusable rockets have moved from concept to engineering, a supply-side shift that should materially lower costs and speed deployment of LEO satellite internet constellations. Routine rocket recovery is likely to accelerate roll-out of large constellations and reduce construction cost pressure. Key areas to watch for supply-chain and investment flow: satellite payloads (including antennas, payload integration, laser‑communication terminals, solar arrays and power systems); rocket components (engines — incl. 3D printing — and airframe structures); ground equipment (civilian terminals and direct‑to‑phone connectivity); and operators/servicers holding scarce regulatory qualifications. The report is constructive on the commercial space sector as market risk sentiment improves.

【加拿大山火持續 美多地釋出空氣質量、強風暴、洪水等預警】受加拿大山火的濃煙影響，截至當地時間7月18日早晨，從美國上中西部、五大湖地區一直延伸到新英格蘭地區，再向南覆蓋東北部和中大西洋地區的十餘州已釋出空氣質量警報。據悉，目前加拿大有900多處山火仍在燃燒，大量煙霧持續飄向美國東北部和中大西洋地區。不過，一個將在18日經過的風暴系統有望幫助驅散部分煙霾。與此同時，在西北風的推動下，一股濃煙帶正進入美國中西部和五大湖上游地區，預計當地空氣質量將在整個週末維持較差水平。另一方面，美國風暴預測中心已對俄亥俄河谷、東北部和中大西洋部分地區釋出三級（最高五級）強對流天氣風險預警。95號州際公路沿線的紐約、費城、巴爾的摩和華盛頓特區等主要城市都可能受到影響，匹茲堡、克利夫蘭和布法羅也預計將遭遇強雷暴。多輪降雨和雷暴將帶來破壞性大風、冰雹、少量龍捲風，部分地區有山洪暴發的風險。紐約市、費城等美國東北部部分地區已釋出洪水監測預警，有效期持續至18日晚間。

【Turing Quantum launches industry-grade quantum-classical hybrid agent platform QAgent】July 18 — Turing Quantum unveiled QAgent at the 2026 World Artificial Intelligence Conference (WAIC). The company says QAgent is the world’s first photonic-quantum-based, industry-deployable quantum-classical hybrid agent platform, exposing large language models and agents as a single scheduling interface. Built on Turing Quantum’s proprietary photonic quantum computing stack, QAgent integrates a hybrid quantum-classical compute layer, the DeepQuantum quantum AI programming framework, a quantum algorithm library and industry toolkits, and supports an end-to-end workflow from natural-language input and task decomposition to coordinated quantum-agent invocation, quantum compute scheduling and result aggregation. Turing positions the product as a step toward moving quantum computing from frontier research capacity to industry capability and agent-driven commercial services.

【摩爾執行緒聯合創始人王東：推理市場沒有“萬能晶片”，而是解決方案的組合】GPU廠商摩爾執行緒聯合創始人、執行總裁王東說，“大模型的發展在國內外都非常迅速，現在頭部廠商平均每兩個月完成一次前沿基礎模型版本迭代，而在模型的呼叫成本上，我們發現，中國前沿基礎模型較國外同等智力水平模型有明顯成本優勢，中國模型價效比更優，這也恰恰說明模型公司在有限算力的情況下，對如何提升模型效率、價格效率以及訓練成本方面做了非常多的工作。”王東指出，推理市場沒有“萬能晶片”，而是“解決方案”的組合，“推理市場的技術應用門檻相對較低，且場景高度碎片化，不存在任何一家公司能夠壟斷所有細分應用場景；沒有絕對完美的單一硬體，透過靈活的軟硬協同，每個模型都能找到最適合它的硬體組合，實現成本與效能的最佳平衡。市場將湧現大量ISP公司，為MaaS提供商或終端客戶提供更具價效比，更靈活的定製化推理服務。”

【廣汽埃安關於部分搭載177Ah電池車輛質保服務升級的公告】近期,我們收到行駛里程超過15萬公里的AION S部分使用者反饋動力電池使用出現故障的問題,給大家造成了不便與顧慮,我們深表歉意!廣汽埃安高度重視此問題,透過初步調查發現,部分搭載中創新航177Ah磷酸鐵鋰電池用於營運用途的AION S 車輛出現了電池故障。針對搭載中創新航177Ah磷酸鐵鋰電池的車型,廣汽埃安從即日起採取以下措施:一是將使用該款電池的車輛的電池質保期從8年15萬公里延長至8 年30萬公里;二是加強電池大資料監測,對於發現的異常情況,我們第一時間聯絡使用者,邀約回店提供免費檢測, 根據結果提供免費維修或免費更換電池包服務,廣汽埃安全國銷售服務網點為使用者提供上述服務。三是因電池維修或更換電池包導致車輛營運受影響的,根據國家相關法規,為維修時間超過5天的車主發放補貼。

### 2026-07-18 16:36:00  #公司

【騰訊首次升級釋出具身智慧全棧佈局】7月18日，在2026世界人工智慧大會（WAIC）上，騰訊正式升級釋出具身智慧全棧方案，該方案貫穿雲底座、模型層、平臺層與應用層，全面助力機器人本體及系統開發商提質提效。會上，騰訊首次系統性展示了具身智慧全棧佈局，實現從基座模型、原生智慧體到開發平臺的全面躍升。在模型層，騰訊持續投入基礎研發，構建起涵蓋Hy-Embodied-VLA-0.5、Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0與Hy-Embodied-RxBrain-1.0的具身模型矩陣。目前，上述模型已被應用於真實工業場景，並在導購導覽、養老服務等多個任務中完成落地驗證，藉助軟硬一體的快速適配能力，同一套模型可支撐多形態機器人規模化應用。具身智慧體方面，騰訊同步釋出TairosAgent具身智慧體框架與Apexio智慧體，可將“左右腦”“大腦”“小腦”和身體整合成完整的系統，讓機器人形成一個持續感知、持續決策、持續行動的整體。於2025年釋出的鈦螺絲（Tairos）平臺也迎來升級，將為行業提供模型、智慧體與開發工具等一系列開源能力與服務，降低從模型到本體再到應用全鏈條的開發門檻。此外，在基礎設施能力上，騰訊雲圍繞具身智慧搭建從算力底座、模型服務到感知互動的三層體系，打造覆蓋研發、訓練與交付的具身智慧雲+AI底座。騰訊雲還攜手行業夥伴，打造業內首個雲端EaaS（Embodied-AI-as-a-Service）具身智慧服務，助力加速具身智慧從技術創新走向規模化應用。（上證報）

【阿里與榮耀共創面向手機場景的解決方案，已落地Robot Phone】7月18日，在WAIC 2026“從數字螢幕到具身智慧，開啟物理世界新正規化”分論壇，阿里巴巴宣佈聯合榮耀，基於“千問大模型”共創面向手機場景深度最佳化的解決方案，已在榮耀的Robot Phone落地。未來的手機Agent，核心問題是完成複雜長程任務，比如“預訂下週三上海參加智造大會的行程”，看似日常，但對於手機Agent來說並不容易。Agent需先結合使用者偏好，訂車程短的靠窗二等座高鐵票、挑選離會場最近的酒店；如果車票售罄，Agent需重新規劃，切換到臨近的車次；使用者上傳大會日程表的照片後，Agent 需看懂圖片、掃描二維碼、填寫表單報名。全程涉及意圖補全、記憶注入、skill呼叫、異常恢復和多工具複雜操作等，任何一個環節不穩健，都會導致任務失敗。阿里巴巴集團副總裁、ATH事業群Token Foundry多模態互動負責人許主洪博士在圓桌論壇中表示，在手機Agent火熱的當下，最需要做的“第一件事”，正是找到一個高頻、有價值的真實場景，構建模型-Harness工程-真實場景反饋的Agent閉環，讓模型被大量使用者真正用起來。（硬AI）

【山西省退役軍人事務廳原一級巡視員範波濤被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站7月18日通報，據山西省紀委監委訊息：日前，經山西省委批准，山西省紀委監委對山西省退役軍人事務廳原一級巡視員範波濤嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，範波濤背離初心使命，喪失黨性原則，無視中央八項規定精神，收受禮品、禮金，接受可能影響公正執行公務的旅遊安排；違反組織原則，在組織談話函詢時不如實說明問題，在職務調整方面為他人謀取利益並收受財物；不正確履職，推動有關景區綜合整改整治工作不力，造成不良影響；利用職務上的便利以及職權或地位形成的便利條件，為他人在工程承攬、企業經營等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。範波濤嚴重違反黨的組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經山西省紀委常委會會議研究並報山西省委批准，決定給予範波濤開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其享受的待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【張國清趕赴重慶彭水縣指導山體崩塌災害現場搜救和應急處置工作時強調 全力科學搜救 嚴防次生災害 細緻做好受災群眾安置和基本生活保障】根據習近平總書記重要指示和李強總理要求，中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理張國清代表黨中央、國務院，17日晚率有關部門負責同志趕赴重慶彭水縣指導山體崩塌災害現場搜救和應急處置工作。張國清抵達後立即前往山體崩塌災害現場，詳細瞭解救援進展和困難。18日早晨，張國清再次來到災害現場研究最佳化救援方案，併到縣人民醫院、臨時安置點看望受傷人員和轉移安置群眾，轉達習近平總書記的關懷牽掛。隨後主持召開會議，傳達習近平總書記重要指示批示精神和李強總理批示、視訊通話要求，研究部署支援幫助重慶搶險救援工作。張國清強調，要千方百計搜救失聯人員，針對災害現場救援作業空間受限、次生災害風險大、連續降雨等情況，強化現場指揮和安全管控，組織專業救援力量和適用搶險救援裝備開展搜救。周圍山體仍存在坍塌風險，要強化邊坡和殘留滑坡體實時監測預警與救援聯動，嚴防救援過程中發生次生災害。要竭盡全力救治受傷群眾，用心用情細緻做好遇難和失聯人員家屬安撫工作，加強心理疏導，保障好轉移群眾基本生活。他強調，近期防災減災救災形勢嚴峻，極端天氣多發頻發，各地區各有關方面要樹牢底線思維、極限思維，進一步壓緊壓實隱患排查、預警叫應、轉移避險、救援救助等各環節責任措施。要緊盯臨山臨江、切坡建房等高風險點位，加強專業巡查排險，充分發揮基層網格員作用，發現險情堅決果斷轉移，切實保障人民群眾生命財產安全。（新華社）

【Iran proposes environmental service fee for ships transiting Strait of Hormuz】Fars News reports Iran’s Department of Environment international affairs director has submitted implementation rules to government to levy an environmental service fee on vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz. Fees would be set according to vessel type, cargo, transit record and assessed environmental-risk level. Officials cite UNCLOS and related treaties as legal basis, saying coastal states may intervene and charge for services if innocent passage is breached or if national security, public health or the environment are threatened; chargeable services are described as public-health and environmental protection measures. The draft does not specify payment mechanisms; detailed execution will be determined by the competent authorities, with proceeds intended to fund navigational safety and sustainable transit in the Strait.

【FCA's new aggregated short-position disclosures contain errors, raising market doubts】The Financial Conduct Authority this week published short-position data under its new regime, which replaces name-level disclosure of significant short holders with aggregated per-stock totals, the Financial Times reports. Data provider Breakout Point identified multiple clear errors: positions later deleted or altered with no visible amendment record, and some entries that appear to be years-old and unlikely to still exist. 【世界盃周邊IP熱銷刷屏，義烏商二代仍在海外展會冷靜接單】7月17日，義烏創二代、浙江蓓蕾布藝有限公司負責人駱添樂正在泰國的展會上忙碌地接待客商的詢價。他告訴第一財經，這是公司第一次如此大範圍地參與世界盃IP的定製。他們與授權方聚星動力成立合資企業——後者手握8支國家隊及8家豪門俱樂部的全品類授權，而蓓蕾布藝負責全部毛絨類產品的設計、生產與製造，雙方共享銷售權益。這款產品是本屆世界盃周邊的熱賣款之一。雖然有熱賣款，但駱添樂坦言，世界盃的整體銷量不達預期。這與熱搜上“全網買不到”的圖景形成反差。對於供應端而言，面對全球經濟波動下的需求變化，“世界盃”帶給市場的熱度不容置疑，但這種熱度的傳導並不一定能均勻地抵達供應鏈的每一環。

【Scotiabank: ECB likely to hold rates in July amid elevated uncertainty】Scotiabank expects the ECB to hold rates in July, arguing policy need not move in a straight line during periods of high uncertainty. Markets have priced a 25bp June hike and expect the deposit rate to be left at 2.25% thereafter, with consensus leaning to stand pat in July. ECB President Lagarde said on July 1: "I think the upside risks to inflation and downside risks to growth may be more balanced than a few weeks ago, because things are changing fast." Since then US‑Iran tensions have flared and WTI and Brent have each risen roughly $12/barrel. June euro‑area CPI fell 0.1% MoM and core CPI eased YoY to 2.4% from 2.6%, which may give the ECB more time to assess under its data‑dependent framework; market sentiment indicators nonetheless remain overall tilted hawkish.

【CAICT: China's daily token calls up more than 1,000x】The China Academy of Information and Communications Technology (CAICT) said daily token calls in China rose from about 100 bln at the start of 2024 to 140 tln by end‑March 2026, an increase of over 1,000x. CAICT vice president Wei Liang said tokens — the units large models use to process information and measure model invocation cost — are spawning a new token economy. The multi‑round model invocations driven by agent-scale applications are sharply increasing AI compute demand and are prompting the development of token metering, scheduling, pricing and trading models.

### 2026-07-18 12:34:54  #其他

【中國足協釋出有關申思等“禁足”人員的情況通報】中國足協18日釋出有關申思等“禁足”人員的情況通報，原文如下:

近日，中國足球協會關注到有關申思、祁宏參與足球青訓活動相關事件的資訊，現通報如下情況：

1.《關於對原上海中遠匯麗足球俱樂部運動員申思等四人違規違紀行為的處罰決定》（足紀字〔2013〕028號）已註明，中國足球協會終身禁止申思、祁宏從事任何與足球有關的活動。

2.《中國足球協會紀律準則》第二十四條針對“禁止從事任何與足球有關的活動”作明確解釋：禁止從事任何與足球有關的活動是指禁止被處罰物件從事任何與足球有關的活動（包括但不限於投資、管理、比賽或其他）。

3.中國足球協會將密切關注上海市有關部門對事件的調查進展，調查結果將及時向社會通報。

### 2026-07-18 12:11:25  #行業

【崔東樹：鋰電池消費稅調整，是對整車企業造電池的有力助推，有利於中國汽車產業做大做強】乘聯分會秘書長崔東樹認為：鋰電池消費稅調整，是對整車企業造電池的有力助推，有利於中國汽車產業做大做強。我對此的核心判斷是：這次政策調整意味著“油電同權”的大幕正式拉開。從購置稅減半、車船稅免徵取消，到電池消費稅恢復徵收，新能源汽車的稅收優惠“保護期”正在分階段、有節奏地退出。燃油車長期承擔全額車船稅與燃油稅，插混、增程式及新能源商用車卻享受免稅紅利，形成“有排放、用道路、獲收益卻不承擔稅負”的漏洞。油電同權不是“一刀切”均等化，而是依據技術屬性、排放特徵、使用場景，建立權責匹配、稅負公平的汽車稅制體系。

### 2026-07-18 12:10:43  #MKT News #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #Chip Wars #S&P500 #Impact bearish #Nasdaq100 #Nikkei 225

【Unresolved doubts driving the equity sell-off】Markets are increasingly doubting prior certainties, a dynamic amplifying the current equity decline. The S&P 500 fell 1.6% this week, the Nasdaq Composite dropped 2.9% and the Nasdaq-100 lost 2.7%, putting July on track to be the worst in over 20 years. The Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is down about 20% from its June 22 peak and entered bear-market territory on Friday. Asian pain is visible: KOSPI is off more than 25% and Japan’s Nikkei 225 plunged 4% on Friday, entering a technical correction. The sell-off lacks a single clear catalyst; cited drivers include concerns that chip-sector gains in 1H ran too far, large tech borrowing to fund capex plans, and persistent macro uncertainty under new Fed policy. BTIG chief market technical analyst Jonathan Krinsky warned a summer-style correction similar to 2024 could push the S&P 500 below its 200-day moving average at 6,983 points. If that occurs, semiconductors would likely weaken further and recent breadth trades and the Magnificent Seven could stall and turn down. Market action appears to be moving in that direction despite the absence of an obviously dangerous headline.

【塔可鍾宣佈完成全美供應鏈受影響生菜的撤換，應對環孢子蟲病事件】據美國疾病控制和預防中心最新資料顯示，美國目前已有5個州共報告了1644例環孢子蟲病感染病例，相關患者均表示發病前曾在美國快餐連鎖店塔可鍾（Taco Bell）的門店用餐。美國食品和藥物管理局於7月16日表示，針對該起疫情的調查顯示，感染源可能與該快餐連鎖店部分門店從墨西哥進口的球生菜有關。針對這一突發的食品安全事件，塔可鍾7月17日宣佈，截至今日，全美所有門店已全面完成對受影響的泰勒農場（Taylor Farms）生菜的撤換工作，目前該受影響的原料已從全國供應鏈中徹底清除。

【聯想集團副總裁陳敏儀：AI不應是少數人的奢侈品，而應該成為像水和電一樣的基礎設施】聯想集團副總裁、SSG業務應用服務交付部負責人陳敏儀日前在上海舉辦的WAIC“共贏金磚”論壇上指出，AI的終極價值不是技術本身的高度，而是技術惠及世界的廣度。AI不應是少數人的奢侈品，而應該成為像水和電一樣的基礎設施。她稱，當前人工智慧技術成為推動產業升級和社會經濟發展的核心生產力。依託自研技術，聯想為本屆世界盃量身定製了多項AI技術方案，這些技術應用不僅推動AI重塑體育行業，更實踐和驗證了AI在技術平權、消費體驗提質和全要素管理等政策導向上的真實價值，並且探索出一條AI普惠的新正規化：從頂級場景驗證，到標準化方案沉澱，再到規模化複製推廣，這是聯想推動AI普惠的核心方法論。

### 2026-07-18 09:54:57  #MKT News #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #WTI #DXY

【Assessment: Iran escalates strikes on US bases in Middle East; Jordan bases take heaviest damage】Open-source intelligence group Faytuks Network reports Iran has stepped up missile strikes on US bases over the past week. Most missiles were intercepted and most Gulf bases sustained only minor damage, but strikes in Jordan have been more persistent and damaging. Despite interceptions, missiles have hit Jordanian targets nearly nightly since the escalation, a pattern that may indicate local air defenses are approaching saturation and interceptor shortages, which would reduce per-shot intercept effectiveness. Muwaffaq Salti Air Base — a key US power-projection node hosting multiple fighter squadrons, including F-15s and F-35s — has sustained multiple hits; satellite imagery shows two new impact points near a northern hangar and several destroyed structures. King Hussein (H5) Air Base has recorded at least two hits; imagery shows a destroyed hangar that may have housed aircraft (presence unknown) and craters and burn marks near the runway edge, implying damage to adjacent runway areas. Faytuks says most other attacks on US facilities have largely failed and so far had limited operational impact.

【古井貢古20領跌 白酒大單品總價連續五天重新整理近四個月新低】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售均價7月18日整體繼續微幅回落，深陷低位連創新低。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9805元，較昨日微跌1元，連續第七天回落，並連續第五日重新整理今年3月22日以來最低。縱觀全場，核心單品的漲跌分化依然顯著，終端交投量能基本穩定，總價低位震盪下行態勢延續。

今日11大白酒單品五漲五跌一平，漲跌家數均衡，但輸家跌幅略佔上風。上漲方面，青花郎漲幅居前，上漲5元/瓶，重返690元關口上方；飛天茅臺、五糧液普五八代、青花汾20與洋河夢之藍M6+均上漲3元/瓶，其中飛天茅臺連續兩日回升，但仍處於近四個月低位，隨著i茅臺平臺零售價上調至1639元/瓶，其相對官方定價的價差收窄至79元/瓶；五糧液普五八代終結此前連續四日的快速下探，自“酒價內參”去年11月有統計以來最低位處小幅修復，但仍處於780元關口下方；青花汾20連續兩日回升；洋河夢之藍M6+自近一月最低位連續兩日反彈，進一步向590元關口靠近。下跌方面，古井貢古20跌幅居前，下跌6元/瓶，連續兩日回落並失守530元關口，創7月份以來最低；國窖1573與習酒君品均下跌4元/瓶，其中後者價格再度跌破630元關口；精品茅臺與五糧液1618均下跌2元/瓶，其中後者重新失守820元關口。水晶劍南春價格環比昨日保持不變。

### 2026-07-18 07:23:23  #宏觀

【經濟日報：如何化解“車大位小”困局】近期，不少車主反映遭遇了“車大位小”的停車難題，想停進車位不容易，想開關車門也不方便。經濟日報刊文指出，化解“車大位小”困局，當務之急是統一標準、明確底線。目前，國內公共停車位的建設寬度尺寸主要是依據住建部2015年印發的《車庫建築設計規範》，該規範設計的小型車標準車位寬度僅為2.4米，而當前主流新能源SUV車身寬度普遍接近2米，兩車並排後剩餘空間極為有限。同時，該標準為推薦性標準，實際執行中常被打折扣。2024年，住建部已啟動《城市停車專項規劃標準》起草工作，新標準將更加強調車位對新能源汽車的適配性。在標準修訂過程中，應充分徵求各方意見，對新建專案設定硬性底線，對存量改造給予彈性空間。化解“車大位小”困局，還需系統統籌，推進長短期綜合施策。短期來看，可對具備條件的車位進行改造，滿足大車的停車需求。長期來看，推動城市規劃、住建、交通等部門建立協同機制，將車位配建標準納入土地出讓條件、專案驗收等硬約束。同時，在汽車生產和推介過程中，應進一步最佳化產品設計，強化輿論引導，破除“車大即高階”的片面認知，為消費者提供尺寸更合理、效能更均衡的汽車產品。

3、美國總統特朗普發文指責加拿大維護森林不善，給美國帶來“數十億美元”損失。他表示當天將就此致電加拿大總理，並威脅說美方損失必須加到加拿大支付的關稅上。
4、當地時間17日，伊朗最高領袖軍事顧問穆赫辛·雷扎伊表示，美國違反了美伊諒解備忘錄的條款，如今這份備忘錄已名存實亡。

6、數名來自美國和以色列的訊息人士稱，美國政府已“通知”以色列，將向以色列增派數十架空中加油機，後續可能擴大對伊朗的軍事行動。訊息同時指出，以總理內塔尼亞胡將對此作出最終決定。
7、Meta正與Anthropic展開初步磋商，擬向後者出租其資料中心的算力。這項合作將為時兩年，價值最高可達100億美元。

9、阿斯麥控股將於2027年1月1日向全球員工發放一次性2萬歐元（約合22862美元）獎勵，前提是員工在整個歸屬期內持續任職，該獎勵將於2030年初完成歸屬。

Colombia Consumer Confidence Jumps in June — Colombia’s Consumer Confidence Index rose 6.5 percentage points to 24.3% in June 2026 from 17.8% in May. The Consumer Expectations Index climbed to 31.4% in June from 26.7% in May, while the Current Economic Conditions Index rose to 13.8% from 4.4%. Consumers became more optimistic about both their household finances and the country's economic outlook. The share of respondents expecting "good economic times" over the next 12 months increased to 31.3% from 21.1%, while expectations for an improvement in the national economy rose to 22.5% from 16.8%. Households also reported a better assessment of their current financial situation. The balance of respondents who said their household's financial condition was better than a year earlier increased to 11.5% from 3.1%. Meanwhile, willingness to purchase durable goods jumped to 16.1% from 5.8%.

### 2026-07-18 04:25:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #Mexico #Currency #Importance 1

Mexican Peso Retreats as Risk Aversion Boosts Dollar — The Mexican peso weakened to 17.52 per USD in July after reaching 17.40 earlier in the month, as escalating tensions in the Middle East fueled risk aversion across global financial markets. The escalation prompted investors to seek safe-haven assets such as the US dollar. Meanwhile, Mexico's industrial output contracted by more than expected in May, with weakness broad-based across sectors, adding pressure to the peso amid signs of a slowing economy. Oil prices also extended gains as tensions near the Strait of Hormuz intensified, fueling energy-driven inflation fears. In contrast, Mexico's annual inflation rate eased to 3.37% in June 2026 from 3.94% in May, the lowest reading since December 2020. The figure came in slightly below market expectations of 3.52% and remained within the Bank of Mexico's target tolerance range of one percentage point around 3%.

【美銀：關鍵指標極度看漲，建議拋售股票】美國銀行由邁克爾·哈特內特（Michael Hartnett）領導的策略師團隊表示，資產配置者已變得極度看漲，這通常是市場的一個警告訊號，表明投資者應該“減少股票敞口、撤退或進行板塊輪動”，而不是繼續加倉風險資產。 哈特內特在週五的一份報告中寫道，美國銀行的內部情緒指標——“牛熊指標”（Bull & Bear Indicator）正在閃爍逆向的“賣出訊號”。這主要歸因於“機構現金水平驟降、強勁的股票資金流入”以及全球股指廣度的增強。該指標已攀升至9.6，進一步推入極度看漲區域。而在大約兩個月前，該指標讀數為8.0，這通常意味著市場因擁擠的看漲倉位而過熱。美銀表示，該指標低於2.0的閾值意味著買入訊號。

After reviewing new strike options, Trump is said to be weighing a broader operation beyond current strikes around the Strait of Hormuz and could order an escalation within days. The US currently positions roughly 30 military tankers each at Ben-Gurion Airport (near Tel Aviv) and Ramon Airport (southern Israel). Israeli officials say Washington aims to send additional dozens of tankers in the coming days to restore levels seen early in the conflict and prefers operating from Ben-Gurion because other Israeli airbases are viewed as more vulnerable to Iranian attack.

European Stocks Close Lower — European stocks closed sharply lower on Friday, tracking global equity markets as investors continued to pivot out of the AI trade and geopolitical tensions escalated. The Euro STOXX 50 dropped 1.1% to 6,229 and the STOXX Europe 600 fell 0.4% to 641. AI-related companies tanked across the world amid lingering concerns that AI hyperscalers are due to slow spending on infrastructure. This was magnified by the release of strong LLM models by Chinese firm Moonshot, risking a pivot away from Western technology. ASML slipped 3.9%, while Siemens tanked 2.3%. In the meantime, pro-inflationary risks from higher natural gas prices pressured the credit outlook and equity for the main banks. BBVA and Deutsche Bank slipped around 2.5% each. Meanwhile, UniCredit lost 2.5% after the German government signaled some willingness to discuss acquisition terms with the Italian lender for the tender of its Commerzbank stake.

US Dollar Index Holds Bounce — The US dollar index rose to 100.8, rebounding from the one-month low of 100.5 on July 16th and diverging with the retreat for Treasury yields amid a pivot to safer assets. Trade jitters with China retook the spotlight after President Trump claimed that China interfered with the US 2020 presidential elections. The claims jeopardized their truce in economic relations since the exchange of tariffs softened last year, magnifying ongoing shipping bottlenecks amid the war in the Middle East. Trade disputes were also featured between the EU and China, dampening demand for the Euro. On the monetary policy front, FOMC members continued to signal that evidence of stubborn core inflation may warrant a rate hike this year, backed by recent evidence of strong retail sales and low unemployment claim levels. The hawkish remarks prevailed despite soft inflation data for June and a second decline in inflation expectations per the Michigan consumer confidence survey.

【香港特區政府歡迎立法會透過《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例草案》 】香港特區政府17日表示，歡迎特區立法會當日透過《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例草案》。草案的順利透過，是落實中央相關授權的重要里程碑，為在重建後的皇崗口岸實施“一地兩檢”安排提供法律基礎，更標誌著口岸開通的籌備工作邁出關鍵一步。 國務院於7月8日就啟用皇崗口岸港方口岸區及相關延伸區有關事項作出批覆，確定港方口岸區的具體座標和麵積，並同意港方口岸區由2026年7月31日0時起啟用並依照香港特別行政區法律實施管轄。為確保中央的授權得以全面落實，特區政府有責任於7月31日前完成相關的本地立法工作。 據悉，《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例》將於7月20日刊憲，並於7月31日生效，為特區在皇崗口岸設立港方口岸區，以及在港方口岸區依法實施管轄提供法律基礎。（新華社）

### 2026-07-17 22:33:58  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #S&P500 #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact mixed

【Dollar hedging costs fall to lowest level this year】Cost of hedging dollar volatility has dropped to its lowest level this year. This week the one‑month implied volatility on the dollar spot index fell to its lowest since December, reversing much of the spike after the March outbreak of the Iran war. Despite Fed policy uncertainty and renewed Middle East tensions, traders signal a very low probability of a major shock to the global reserve currency. The calm is reinforcing a key market theme: US equity resilience and reduced currency volatility are drawing flows into arbitrage and carry trades that profit from interest‑rate differentials when FX and risk appetite are stable. ING FX strategist Francesco Pesole called the fall in dollar volatility striking and said AI‑driven equity resilience appears to be stabilising exchange rates and sustaining a self‑reinforcing low‑volatility, carry‑dominated environment; he added that a tech sell‑off would likely not deter demand for these strategies.

### 2026-07-17 22:32:49  #MKT News

【Lithium batteries to face consumption tax like refined fuel】Broker analysts say a consumption tax on lithium batteries was widely anticipated and industry expects limited impact. Battery cell prices are about 0.35–0.40 yuan/Wh; a 2% consumption tax would add roughly 0.007–0.008 yuan/Wh. A 2–4% tax range corresponds to an equivalent 12,000–25,000 yuan/ton effect on lithium carbonate. Lithium carbonate prices have been rising and orders remain supply-constrained, so the tax’s incremental burden is small. For end buyers, a 2–4% levy would raise per‑vehicle costs by only a few hundred yuan, well below prior lithium-driven cost swings. CPCA secretary-general Cui Dongshu said the move signals that “parity between oil and electricity” is being established; together with earlier purchase‑tax cuts and the removal of vehicle-and-vessel tax exemptions, the sector’s tax preferences are being phased out.

【China's Ministry of Agriculture launches 100-day campaign to protect autumn grain during peak flood season】Entering the main flood period of late July–early August, when agricultural disasters and autumn-grain yield formation are most acute, the Ministry of Agriculture has launched a 100-day field-management and disaster-resilience campaign to secure the autumn and annual grain harvests. The drive targets key crops, vulnerable regions and major disaster events with crop-, region- and stage-specific measures: scientific fertilization, water-saving irrigation, foliar sprays and other yield-enhancement field practices. It directs ditch clearing and soil-moisture management, chemical measures to control lodging and single foliar-spray operations intended to deliver multiple protective effects. Authorities will implement unified and joint prevention-and-control and emergency-response arrangements to minimise disaster losses and protect harvests.

US 10-Year Yield Declines — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note fell to 4.52% from the near two-month high of 5.62% on July 13th, amid softer inflation data and a decline in risk sentiment. Both consumer and producer inflation decline in June, while inflation expectations measured by the Michigan survey dropped for a second month, suggesting that the pullback in wholesale fuel costs are being transmitted to the economy. Fixed-income assets were also supported by new geopolitical uncertainty after US President Trump claimed China compromised US presidential elections in 2020, risking the truce between both countries since the exchange of tariff hikes last year. Still, pro-inflationary risks lingered as commercial vessels refrained from crossing the Strait of Hormuz due to new strikes between Iran and the US. Rate futures suggested that over two thirds of the market are positioned for a Fed hike by the end of the year, although this month's decision is expected to be a hold.

### 2026-07-17 22:05:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Consumer Confidence #Importance 3

US Consumer Sentiment Improves for Second Straight Month — The University of Michigan's Consumer Sentiment Index rose to 54.4 in July 2026, beating expectations of 51.0 and marking a second straight monthly increase after May's record low, according to the preliminary estimate. Sentiment reached its highest level since February, supported by easing gasoline prices. All five index components improved, led by roughly 20% gains in buying conditions for durable goods and year-ahead business conditions. The improvement was broad-based across age, income, wealth, and political groups, with particularly strong gains among consumers without a bachelor's degree. Despite the rebound, sentiment remains 12% below its level a year ago as elevated prices continue to weigh on households. Meanwhile, one-year inflation expectations eased to 4.2% from 4.6%, while long-run expectations held steady at 3.3%. Most survey responses were collected before the July 7 resumption of US strikes against Iran and the subsequent rise in gasoline prices.

### 2026-07-17 22:01:50  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent #XAUUSD

【US strikes on Iranian bridges, water plants and granaries broaden targets; analysts flag higher Strait of Hormuz oil and shipping risk】US strikes against Iran have increasingly hit civilian infrastructure — bridges, water-treatment facilities and grain stores — prompting analysts to warn of heightened oil and shipping risk and a broader shift in US targeting. King's College senior lecturer Mark Hilborne says many struck sites are dual-use but clearly affect civilians; he highlights bridges as key to southern military logistics and to Iran's ability to move equipment and threaten shipping. Hilborne adds a northeast bridge struck earlier sits on a strategic trade corridor used for economic traffic and alleged weapons-part shipments. Attacks have concentrated on Bandar Abbas, home to Iran's conventional navy and the IRGC naval base overlooking the Strait of Hormuz; more than 90% of Iran's oil exports transit the strait, making Bandar Abbas both a military and economic choke point. London military analyst Alex Alfirraz Scheers says expanding strikes beyond conventional military targets may signal a wider strategic shift — aimed at enforcing a maritime blockade, degrading military logistics and pressuring Iran to negotiate — and could represent early steps toward preparing limited ground operations. Lancaster University professor Simon Mabon cautions the pattern could be interpreted as a precursor to ground intervention but calls a full-scale invasion unlikely and warns attacks on civilian infrastructure may strengthen Tehran's domestic narrative and raise the risk of further escalation. Market takeaway: strikes widening to infrastructure in southern Iran and at Bandar Abbas raise the risk premium on crude flows and regional shipping security, warranting close monitoring for disruption to exports and any signs of further escalation.

【Rate‑hike bets and fund rotation pull tech leaders lower; semis enter bear market】US stocks opened lower Friday as broad tech weakness hit markets. The Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is more than 20% below its record closing high and looks set to confirm a bear market. SK Hynix ADR fell below its $149 issue price for the first time. SpaceX shares are down about 38% from their peak, erasing roughly $1 trillion of peak value. AMD dropped over 7%, Intel over 6%, and TSMC over 5%. Analysts say the chip rout has exceeded fundamentals and is driven more by fund/style rotation than company-level news. Hawkish signals from Fed officials pushed up rate-hike expectations: Fed chair Kevin Warsh’s critical remarks on inflation at a hearing tightened markets, while other Fed officials signalled hawkish intent — Fed official Logan explicitly called for hikes and Fed official Schmid warned the risk of inflation accelerating in coming months remains. Continued uncertainty over US‑Iran war prospects has also dented risk appetite.

Baltic Dry Index at 2-Week Low — The Baltic Exchange's dry bulk freight index, which monitors rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, retreated for the third session on Friday, slipping about 3.1% to its lowest since July 3 at 2,752 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, also declined for the third day, dropping 5.6% to 4,097 points, as weaker cargo enquiries and increased vessel availability pressured spot earnings. At the same time, the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell by 0.4% to 2,248 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index rose 0.4% to a fresh high since August 2022 at 1,737 points. For the week, the benchmark index was down more than 6%.

【Apple overtakes NVIDIA as world's most valuable company】On Friday Apple (AAPL.O) overtook NVIDIA (NVDA.O) as the world's most valuable company. Apple's intraday market value stood at $4.88 trillion; NVIDIA shares fell 3.5%, putting its market value at about $4.86 trillion. The ranking change reflects investors re-evaluating which firms benefit from the AI boom beyond the most obvious winners and is the first time Apple has held the top spot since April last year. Tony Meadows, chief investment officer at BRI Wealth Management, said Apple had been viewed as an AI laggard for not investing in model development but market sentiment has shifted; he said Apple's lower capex dependence and its ability to monetise AI through services, ecosystem lock‑in and hardware upgrades supports a valuation reset that reflects confidence in earnings sustainability rather than speculative AI upside.

US Stocks Fall Sharply — US stocks fell sharply on Friday amid a fresh selloff for chip producers and new geopolitical tensions. The S&P 500 lost 1.4%, the Nasdaq 100 fell 2.5%, and the Dow lost 400 points. Semiconductor manufacturers plunged on concerns that AI hyperscalers will invest less in AI infrastructure, reversing the rally from this year. Risks of lower capital expenditure were also stemmed from improving Chinese AI models, including the latest Kimi release by Kimi. Micron and Intel dropped more than 4%, while Nvidia lost 2%. Meanwhile, pro-inflationary risks gathered momentum as the war in the Middle East continued and fuel costs picked up. Economic headwinds were also stemmed by US President Trump claiming China interfered in the US 2020 presidential elections, risking the truce between both countries since last year's tariff's exchanges. On the earnings front, Netflix sank 11% after it forecast another quarter of slowing sales.

【此猴非彼猴！這種炒作離大譜】7月17日，“猴”概念股集體降溫。截至當日收盤，益諾思跌超14%，昭衍新藥A股股價跌停，美迪西、康龍化成、藥明康德等熱門“猴”概念股紛紛下挫。 值得注意的是，“猴”概念爆火後，有不少網友將實驗猴炒作成了“猴模組”，把峨眉山稱為“猴儲存”，甚至連帶著給果蔬企業宏輝果蔬都編出了“光刻蕉”的諧音梗。7月15日，宏輝果蔬收穫漲停，有網友給出的解釋是“宏輝果蔬賣香蕉，猴子愛吃香蕉”；以文旅主業聞名的峨眉山A則因景區裡猴子多，被網友強行劃入“猴”概念陣營，近期連續收漲。 這種以諧音、聯想為依據的炒作路徑，或存在市場情緒過熱和非理性炒作的風險。今日，隨著“猴”概念股集體降溫，峨眉山A、宏輝果蔬也隨之一同下挫。（上海證券報微信公眾號）

【U.S. June factory output stalls as durable-goods slump drags】U.S. manufacturing output stalled in June, weighed down by a drop in durable-goods production. Factory output, which accounts for roughly three-quarters of total industrial production, was flat month-on-month in June after May was revised to +0.1%; economists had expected +0.1%. Overall industrial production rose 0.1% for the second consecutive month; utilities and mining each climbed 0.4%. Within durables, output of wood products, machinery, electrical equipment and household appliances fell, while growth in computers & electronics and defense & aerospace slowed. Other recent data show some manufacturing pickup, but renewed U.S.-Iran conflict poses upside risk to energy prices that could raise input costs and suppress demand.

Silver Hits Multi-Month Low on Fed Rate Outlook — Silver fell to around $55 an ounce on Friday, its lowest level since late November 2025, as rising oil prices fueled inflation concerns and reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep interest rates higher for longer. Geopolitical tensions escalated after Iran launched fresh strikes on US facilities in the Middle East, following a sixth consecutive night of US attacks on Iranian military targets that continued to disrupt shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Adding to the pressure, Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan called for a rate hike, while Fed Vice Chair Philip Jefferson said he would support tighter policy if inflation failed to show near-term improvement. Markets now assign roughly a 50% probability to a September rate increase. Meanwhile, data released during the week showed US consumer and producer prices both declined in June, largely reflecting lower energy costs, while import prices unexpectedly increased.

### 2026-07-17 21:30:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Capacity Utilization #Importance 1

US Capacity Utilization Stable for Third Month — US capacity utilization stood at 76.1% in June 2026, unchanged from the previous month following a downward revision and slightly below market expectations of 76.2%. The reading marked a third consecutive month of stable capacity utilization, remaining 3.3 percentage points below its long-run average for 1972–2025. Among the major sectors, the operating rate for utilities edged up 0.2 percentage points to 69.5%, though it remained 14.5 percentage points below its historical average. Mining utilization also increased, rising 0.4 percentage points to 87.4%. By contrast, manufacturing capacity utilization edged down to 75.7%, remaining 2.5 percentage points below its long-run average.

【中國貿促會發布關於2026年APEC工商領導人峰會參會代表組織邀請工作的宣告】中國貿促會發文稱，2026年APEC工商領導人峰會將於11月在深圳舉辦。近期，中國國際貿易促進委員會（以下簡稱“中國貿促會”）發現有機構和個人冒用我會及我會所屬中國國際商會名義，透過微信、簡訊、郵件等方式擅自開展2026年APEC工商領導人峰會參會代表邀請、招募工作，並收取報名費用及索要個人資訊，牟取非法利益。對此我會鄭重宣告：中國貿促會未委託或授權任何機構或個人開展本屆峰會參會代表的邀請、招募及收費工作。本屆峰會參會代表的邀請工作由中國貿促會直接實施，相關報名、註冊安排僅透過峰會官方網站（www.apecceosummit2026.cn）及中國貿促會官方網站（www.ccpit.org）統一發布。任何非經上述官方渠道釋出的參會邀請、代理報名及收費資訊，請務必謹慎甄別。因輕信非官方渠道資訊而導致的一切損失，由資訊提供者及相關責任人承擔，主辦方恕不負責。對冒充中國貿促會或假借受我會委託名義實施不實宣傳、非法牟利等不法行為的機構和個人，我會保留依法追究其法律責任的權利。

【Sources: Pakistan in talks with Kuwait to expand defense pact】Pakistan is negotiating with Kuwait to expand bilateral defense cooperation in exchange for energy cooperation and investment, five people with knowledge of the talks said. The sources said discussions are at an early stage and could be complicated if US‑Iran tensions escalate. They added Islamabad is increasingly concerned its defense pact with Saudi Arabia signed last year could draw Pakistan into a US‑Iran conflict. After a Houthi attack on Saudi Arabia on Monday, nuclear‑armed Pakistan told Iran it would view attacks on Saudi territory as attacks on Pakistan. Kuwait has suffered multiple severe attacks from Iran this year; any defense agreement with Kuwait would raise questions about Pakistan’s role as a potential mediator between Washington and Tehran, the sources said.

Gold Posts Weekly Loss as Rising Oil Prices Boost Rate Expectations — Gold fell to around $3,970 an ounce on Friday and was down more than 3% for the week, as escalating US-Iran tensions drove oil prices higher, fueling inflation concerns and strengthening expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep interest rates elevated. Geopolitical tensions intensified after Tehran launched fresh strikes on US facilities in the Middle East, following a sixth consecutive night of US attacks on Iranian military targets that continued to disrupt traffic through the Strait of Hormuz. Fed officials also reinforced the hawkish outlook. Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan called for another rate hike, while Vice Chair Philip Jefferson said he would support tighter policy if inflation failed to improve in the near term. Markets are now pricing in roughly a 50% chance of a September rate increase. On the data front, US consumer and producer prices both declined in June, largely due to lower energy costs, while import prices unexpectedly rose.

【市場監管總局出手嚴管拼豆！面向14歲以下兒童使用的“拼豆”產品：拼豆包裝須標註適用年齡】央視財經記者今天（17日）瞭解到，為保障消費者人身健康安全，依照《中華人民共和國產品質量法》《中華人民共和國未成年人保護法》等法律法規，市場監管總局釋出“拼豆”產品質量安全有關事項公告。其中，面向14歲以下兒童使用的“拼豆”產品：應當透過玩具強制性產品認證（CCC認證），不得以“搭售”“贈品”等方式，在玩具產品出廠時違規搭配家用電器；產品應當在顯著位置標註產品適用年齡、安全警示等注意事項，明示家長購買注意事項，提醒家長履行兒童監護責任，按照適用年齡選擇合適玩具，嚴禁模糊標註、虛假標註。 市場監管總局要求：“拼豆”產品生產者應當嚴格按照相關法律法規要求組織生產，加強質量管控。銷售者應當嚴格落實產品質量安全主體責任，履行進貨查驗義務。電商平臺應當加強對平臺內銷售“拼豆”產品的核驗核查，對發現的違規產品立即採取必要處置措施，並依法向市場監管部門報告。

【外交部：將推動世界人工智慧合作組織儘早啟動運轉】7月16日，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定簽署儀式在上海舉行，29個國家代表簽署協定，成為創始成員國。外交部發言人林劍17日下午在例行記者會上介紹有關情況時表示，倡議成立世界人工智慧合作組織是中方推動構建人類命運共同體，落實全球治理倡議的又一生動實踐。下階段，中方將會同各創始成員國推動組織儘早啟動運轉。 記者會上，有記者提問：昨天，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定簽署儀式在上海舉行。中方如何看待成立該組織的意義和影響？該組織同聯合國框架下的人工智慧治理平臺是什麼關係？ 林劍介紹，7月16日，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定的簽署儀式在上海舉行。中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅出席並代表中國政府簽署協定。哈薩克、寮國、巴基斯坦、俄羅斯、印度尼西亞等29個國家代表簽署協定，成為創始成員國。聯合國秘書長古特雷斯等國家和國際組織代表出席儀式。 林劍表示，中方始終積極踐行習近平主席提出的《全球人工智慧治理倡議》，秉持以人為本、向上向善理念，推動人工智慧全球治理。正如習近平主席今天上午在2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式上指出的，世界人工智慧合作組織在上海應運而生，這是中方響應全球南方呼聲，團結國際社會，積極推動人工智慧發展和治理的重大舉措，將成為人工智慧發展史上的一個重要里程碑。 林劍說，中方一貫主張在聯合國框架下推進人工智慧全球治理，推動聯合國大會協商一致透過人工智慧能力建設國際合作決議，支援落實全球數字契約和聯合國可持續發展目標。 林劍表示，中方願同各方一道，遵循聯合國憲章宗旨，秉持共商、共建、共享理念，堅持以人為本原則，加強人工智慧發展戰略、治理規則、技術標準的對接協調，促進人工智慧國際合作和全球治理，確保人工智慧朝著有益、安全、公平方向健康有序發展。（新華社）

【US housing starts rebound on multifamily surge】US housing starts rose 19% in June to a 1.43 mln annualized rate, the strongest since March and above economist forecasts, government data showed. Multifamily starts surged about 76% to a 532k annualized rate after a near-40% drop the prior month; single-family starts slipped 0.2%. High home prices and elevated mortgage rates are suppressing single-family demand but appear to be supporting apartment building. Builders report high single-family inventory and weak demand, prompting sales incentives. Forward-looking permits fell 3% to the lowest since March; single-family permits hit a 10-month low and multifamily permits also declined, signalling downside risk to future construction activity.

【美國6月份新屋開工數反彈 多戶型住宅建設激增】美國6月份新屋開工數在前一個月大幅下降後激增，主要受公寓建設反彈推動。週五釋出的官方資料顯示，6月份新屋開工數增加19%，摺合年率為143萬套，創3月份以來最高水平。該資料高於調查中所有經濟學家的預期。多戶住宅開工數增長超過76%，摺合年率為53.2萬套，此前一個月該資料暴跌近40%。與此同時，單戶住宅開工數下降0.2%，在建築商經歷整體低迷的春季之後再次下滑。多戶住宅建設反彈凸顯出月度資料的波動性，尤其是在公寓領域。不過，高房價和高抵押貸款利率一直壓制獨棟住宅需求，而這些因素也可能在支撐公寓需求。與此同時，獨棟住宅建築商總體上一直面臨庫存高企和需求疲軟的局面。這迫使許多建築商透過銷售激勵措施來吸引買家。

【李常官在北京調研養老服務工作】7月16日，民政部黨組書記李常官在北京市調研養老服務體系建設和養老機構安全管理工作。他強調，要深入學習貫徹習近平總書記關於養老服務工作重要論述，全面貫徹落實黨中央決策部署，牢固樹立和踐行正確政績觀，久久為功，下繡花功夫，把老年人關切的事情一件一件辦好，不斷增進老年人福祉。 李常官先後到朝陽區、大興區，深入養老機構、區域養老服務中心等，詳細瞭解養老機構運營、社群嵌入式養老服務、老年助餐、養老機構安全管理等情況，並座談交流。他指出，養老是重要民生事項，既是家事，也是國事，要以更高政治站位推動養老服務改革發展，加快健全三級養老服務網路，用心用情為老年人做好事辦實事解難事。要按照兜得住、管得好的目標，發揮公辦養老機構壓艙石作用，強化特困老年人、經濟困難失能老年人等群體的兜底保障和照護服務。要順應不離家、不離親的意願，大力發展社群嵌入式養老服務，支援養老服務經營主體連鎖化、標準化、品牌化、普惠化發展，將助餐、助浴、助醫等服務向居家社群延伸，讓老年人在“家門口”就能享受到專業化的養老服務。要注重強化黨建引領，探索“黨建+養老”有效模式，把黨的政治優勢和組織優勢轉化為養老服務發展優勢。（中國民政）

### 2026-07-17 20:53:29  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Precious Metals #S&P500 #Impact bearish #WTI #Impact bullish #XAUUSD

【Wealth manager warns of 'generation-level' bear; says US stocks could fall at least 40%】Oxbow Advisors founder Ted Oakley warned US equities could decline at least 40% — and possibly more — in a 'generation-level' bear that could last one to two years. He said valuations sit about three standard deviations above normal and that mean reversion would require a 40–45% drop; chasing a final 6–8% upside now carries roughly 25% downside risk. Oakley flagged semiconductor concentration risk (10–12 names account for roughly half the market) and used Intel's multi-decade recovery after 1999 as a caution against late-cycle buying. Oxbow's equity exposure is slightly above 60% across 45 stocks, with the remainder in short-term Treasuries. The firm is overweight energy and commodities — Oakley said he would be surprised if oil does not return above $100, arguing production-side disruption is worse than widely recognized — and expects gold, miners and silver to rebound next year as momentum unwinds. CIO Chance Finucane said the firm is cautious on 2027 because next year will face a high 1H base, which could slow growth and produce disinflation that is not supportive for risk assets.

【工信部組織召開重點汽車生產企業座談會 要求汽車生產企業堅決抵制非理性競爭、加強產品測試驗證與安全評估】7月17日，工業和資訊化部裝備工業一司組織召開重點汽車生產企業座談會，部署進一步規範汽車產業競爭秩序、提升汽車產品生產一致性和質量安全水平、開展汽車產品安全風險隱患排查和監督檢查等重點工作。會議要求，汽車生產企業要深入貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，堅決抵制非理性競爭，加強產品測試驗證與安全評估，守牢產品生產一致性和質量安全底線。一是要深入開展產品安全風險隱患排查，對照有關管理要求，系統查詢企業自身和關鍵零部件供應商在產品生產一致性、可靠性、耐久性和新技術測試驗證等方面存在的問題，認真做好整改工作，全面加強供應鏈管理，強化生產一致性保證能力。二是要加強產品創新設計測試驗證，充分評估有關安全風險，穩妥推動新技術裝車應用，確保符合有關國家標準要求，以及保障人體健康和人身、財產安全的要求，嚴格履行告知義務，不得誇大和虛假宣傳。三是要加強組合駕駛輔助和自動駕駛功能安全評估，進一步強化企業在功能安全、預期功能安全、網路安全、資料安全、軟體升級等方面的安全保障能力，加強安全事件監測和處置。會議強調，汽車生產企業要牢固樹立“安全第一、質量至上”的理念，切實承擔起保障產品質量安全和維護公共安全、公民生命財產安全的責任，圍繞產品設計驗證、生產製造、售後服務等方面做好風險防範，切實維護消費者合法權益。下一步，工業和資訊化部將會同相關部門深入開展道路機動車輛產品生產一致性和質量提升行動，集中整治突出問題，進一步加強汽車產品創新設計准入審查和測試驗證有關工作，開展道路機動車輛生產企業產品安全保障能力和生產一致性監督檢查，加強道路機動車輛檢驗檢測機構管理，對於存在問題的道路機動車輛生產企業、檢驗檢測機構，依法依規嚴肅處理，堅決守牢汽車產品安全底線。
